  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现可重复、可扩展的超导量子比特制造需要高质量的约瑟夫森结，而单晶硅可用作其介电势垒材料。现有硅加工技术难以在保持高深宽比结构的同时，将硅鳍片减薄至亚10 nm尺度，因此需要一种可控的减薄工艺。

Method: 采用高阻浮区单晶硅晶圆，先进行各向异性湿法刻蚀形成高深宽比硅鳍片；随后开发数字刻蚀工艺，通过反复的快速热退火（RTA）热氧化生成氧化层，再用气相氢氟酸（VHF）刻蚀去除氧化层，逐步减薄硅鳍片至亚10 nm。为减小鳍片锥度，在RTA氧化升温阶段采用N₂/O₂（5:1）气氛并降低升温速率。

Result: 截面扫描透射电子显微镜（STEM）证实硅鳍片厚度可薄至6 nm，但初始各向异性湿法刻蚀导致截面呈锥形。通过N₂/O₂（5:1）气氛与降低升温速率，锥度显著减小，在1.2 μm高的鳍片上厚度变化仅为3 nm，顶部约12 nm、底部约15 nm。

Conclusion: 该工艺为利用现成硅加工技术、以单晶硅作为介电势垒制备约瑟夫森结提供了可行路径，为可重复、可扩展的超导量子比特制造奠定了基础。

Abstract: This work presents a fabrication process to realize sub-10 nm-thick, high-aspect-ratio Si fins using high-resistivity float-zone single-crystal Si wafers. Following anisotropic wet etching, a digital etching process based on repeated rapid thermal annealing (RTA) oxidation and vapor-phase hydrofluoric acid (VHF) etching of the resulting oxide was developed, enabling thinning of Si fins into the sub-10 nm regime. Cross-sectional scanning transmission electron microscopy confirms fin thicknesses as thin as 6 nm, although with a tapered profile resulting from the initial anisotropic wet etch. Tapering of the fin was reduced by using an N{_2}/O{_2} (5:1) ambient together with a reduced ramp rate during the temperature ramping for the RTA oxidation process. A thickness variation of 3 nm along a 1.2 μm tall fin was achieved on a fin with 12 nm near the top and 15 nm near the base. The developed process provides a pathway for fabricating Josephson junctions, setting a foundation toward reproducible and scalable superconducting qubit fabrication with single-crystal Si dielectric barriers using readily available Si processing technology.

</details>


### [97] [Composition-Driven Metal-to-Semiconductor Transition and Enhanced Phonon Transport in B-C substituted Clathrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32894)
*Ghulam Hussain,Dario Massa,Rajibul Islam,Magdalena Birowska,Carmine Autieri,Xiaoguang Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文证明单一的B→C取代可同时改变电子数和重构键合网络/晶体结构，使立方BaB3C3从金属变为四方BaB2C4半导体（带隙0.33 eV），并使晶格热导率从7.6 W/mK提升至18.7 W/mK（约2.5倍），且由各向同性变为各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，同时控制电子结构与热输运的化学设计规则是热管理和能源材料领域追求的目标。作者希望找到一种简便的化学取代策略，能够在调控电子性质的同时增强声子热输运。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）与机器学习原子间势（MLIP，具体为MACE模型）相结合的方法，研究立方BaB3C3及其B→C取代后的四方BaB2C4结构的电子结构、晶格动力学与声子热输运。MACE模型以DFT能量和力为训练目标，复现声子色散与晶格热导率。

Result: B→C取代向B-C骨架每化学式捐赠一个电子，触发强C-C键形成并打开0.33 eV的带隙（金属→半导体转变）。300 K下，热力学稳定的立方BaB3C3具有各向同性的晶格热导率7.6 W/mK；取代后声子色散硬化（频率从约800 cm^-1上移至约900 cm^-1），声子群速度与寿命同时提升，晶格热导率升至18.7 W/mK（约2.5倍增强），并呈现kappa_x = kappa_y > kappa_z的各向异性特征。

Conclusion: 最小的B→C取代是一种可行的策略，能够在改变电子结构（金属→半导体）的同时增强声子热输运，为热管理与能源材料的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Establishing chemical design rules that simultaneously control the electronic structure and thermal transport is a long-sought goal for heat-management and energy materials. Here, we demonstrate that a single B-to-C substitution changes the electron count and simultaneously reconstructs the bonding network and crystal structure, driving a metal-to-semiconductor transition while concurrently enhancing the lattice thermal conductivity. Using density-functional theory (DFT) and machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs), we investigate the electronic structure, lattice dynamics, and phonon thermal transport in cubic BaB3C3 and its B-to-C-substituted tetragonal BaB2C4 structure. The substitution donates one electron per formula unit to the B-C framework, thereby triggering the formation of strong C-C bonds and opening a bandgap of 0.33 eV. The Message Passing Atomic Cluster Expansion (MACE) model reproduces DFT energies and forces, yielding phonon dispersion and lattice thermal conductivity in excellent agreement with DFT benchmarks. Thermodynamically stable cubic BaB3C3 possesses an isotropic lattice thermal conductivity of 7.6 W/mK at 300 K. Substituting B with C hardens the phonon dispersion (evidenced by a frequency upshift from ~800 to ~900 cm^-1) and simultaneously boosts both phonon group velocities and lifetimes. Consequently, the lattice thermal conductivity surges to 18.7 W/mK, representing a remarkable ~2.5-fold enhancement of the in-plane component and a transformation from isotropic to anisotropic behavior, characterized by kappa_x = kappa_y > kappa_z. Our results demonstrate that minimal B-to-C substitution provides a viable strategy to modulate the electronic structure, thereby transforming the crystal from a metal to a semiconductor and concomitantly enhancing phonon thermal transport.

</details>


### [98] [Parity-selected Bethe-Salpeter equation: symmetry-adapted regularization of the exciton-phonon self-energy in polar semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32909)
*Michael O. Atambo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文证明有限温度下激子-声子自能中Fröhlich发散的1s内态散射顶点可通过电子-空穴干涉被解析消除，并提出一种宇称选择的截断方案，使1s内态通道能以极少q点精确计算，揭示其对温度依赖激子位移的控制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极性材料中有限温度Bethe-Salpeter激子-声子自能计算受到1s内态散射顶点Fröhlich发散的瓶颈制约，需大量q点与数值截断，计算代价高且结果受截断影响。

Method: 对束缚电子-空穴对证明1s内态顶点可精确分解为V_el(q)=q·G(q)，其中G(0)∝(m_h−m_e)/(m_h+m_e)，等质量时因宇称保护恒为零；引入多极干涉因子I_n=β_e^n−(−β_h)^n判定奇点仅在偶极允许的态间通道存活；据此构造宇称选择的激子-声子自能截断，并精确处理1s内态通道。

Result: 1s内态通道以可忽略代价精确求值，收敛所需q点减少10^2–10^3倍；此前被数值截断掩盖的正则化1s内态通道实际上主导了质量非对称极性半导体中温度依赖的激子位移。

Conclusion: 通过电子-空穴干涉与宇称选择截断，Fröhlich发散被解析消除，大幅提升收敛效率，并揭示了1s内态通道对温度依赖激子位移的重要物理作用。

Abstract: Finite-temperature Bethe-Salpeter calculations of exciton-phonon self-energies in polar materials are bottlenecked by the Fröhlich divergence of the intra-1s scattering vertex. We show that for a bound electron-hole pair this divergence is analytically eliminated by electron-hole interference: the intra-1s vertex admits the exact factorization $V_{\mathrm{el}}(q)=q\,\mathcal G(q)$ with $\mathcal G(0)\propto(m_h-m_e)/(m_h+m_e)$, vanishing identically for equal masses (parity protection). A multipole interference factor $\mathcal I_n=β_e^{\,n}-(-β_h)^{n}$ determines where the singularity survives-only in dipole-allowed inter-state channels. We formulate a parity-selected truncation of the exciton-phonon self-energy in which the intra-1s channel is evaluated exactly at negligible cost, converging with $10^2$-$10^3$ fewer $q$-points. We find that the regularized intra-1s channel, previously obscured by numerical cutoffs, controls the temperature-dependent exciton shift in mass-asymmetric polar semiconductors.

</details>


### [99] [Native implementation of the machine-learned Skala exchange-correlation functional in CP2K: Unified one-centre reconstruction for molecular and condensed-phase calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34055)
*Johann Pototschnig,Franz Pöschel,Jürg Hutter,Thomas D. Kühne*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在CP2K的GPW/GAPW方法中实现机器学习交换关联泛函Skala，并为其设计专门的GAPW表示，在周期体系中成功保留Skala的分子基准精度，在晶体内聚能、ICE13相对能量与带隙等测试中优于常用半局域泛函并接近杂化泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习交换关联泛函（如Skala）主要面向分子体系开发并依赖分子代码（如GauXC）实现，难以直接迁移到周期性凝聚相体系。作者希望将Skala无损地整合进支持周期边界条件的CP2K（GPW/GAPW框架），使该泛函在晶体、冰相等凝聚态计算中保持分子基准精度，并为未来用周期性多体参考数据训练该类泛函奠定基础。

Method: 将Skala交换关联泛函以原生方式嵌入CP2K的GPW与GAPW方法中，采用联合单中心重构（同时重构密度、密度梯度和动能密度）以保留混合梯度项及光滑与原子局域贡献之间的非局域耦合；引入针对XC专用的GAPW表示，在原子中心网格上分辨快速变化的局域贡献；实现布里渊区积分采样与点群对称性约化。在充分灵活的轨道基组下验证其精度。

Result: 全电子Skala-D3(BJ)计算对晶体CO₂、NH₃和尿素相对扩散蒙特卡洛（DMC）的MAE为1.54 kJ/mol；对DMC-ICE13全部13个冰相，绝对晶格能MAE为1.16 kJ/mol，相对冰Ih的12个相对能量MAE为0.82 kJ/mol；在固定实验几何下15种材料的带隙MAE为0.43 eV，明显优于常用半局域泛函并接近常用杂化泛函；但LC10基准显示其系统低估平衡晶格常数，存在结构性过结合。

Conclusion: 该实现将分子级Skala泛函成功连接至凝聚相电子结构计算，在晶体内聚能、冰相相对能量与带隙预测上表现出色，但存在晶格常数系统低估的过结合问题，为进一步利用周期性多体参考数据开发改进泛函提供了可行平台。

Abstract: We implement the machine-learned Skala exchange-correlation (XC) functional natively in CP2K using its Gaussian and plane-wave (GPW) and Gaussian and augmented-plane-wave (GAPW) methods. A joint one-centre reconstruction of density, density gradients, and kinetic-energy density before functional evaluation preserves mixed gradient terms and nonlocal couplings between smooth and atom-local contributions in all-electron and pseudopotential calculations. An XC-specific GAPW representation resolves rapidly varying local contributions on atom-centred grids, reducing plane-wave requirements for pseudopotential calculations. The implementation includes Brillouin-zone sampling and point-group symmetry reduction. With sufficiently flexible orbital bases, it retains the molecular benchmark accuracy of our earlier GauXC formulation. All-electron Skala-D3(BJ) calculations yield a mean absolute error (MAE) of 1.54 kJ mol$^{-1}$ against diffusion Monte Carlo for crystalline CO$_2$, NH$_3$, and urea, spanning dispersion, quadrupolar electrostatics, and hydrogen bonding. For all thirteen DMC-ICE13 phases, the MAEs are 1.16 kJ mol$^{-1}$ for absolute lattice energies and 0.82 kJ mol$^{-1}$ for the twelve relative energies to ice Ih. At fixed experimental geometries, all-electron/mixed-core calculations give a band-gap MAE of 0.43 eV on a 15-material set, substantially below commonly used semilocal functionals and comparable to widely used hybrid functionals. The LC10 benchmark nevertheless reveals systematically underestimated equilibrium lattice constants, indicating structural overbinding. This framework connects molecular Skala to condensed-phase electronic structure and enables future development using periodic many-body reference data.

</details>


### [100] [Impact of Phonon-Magnon Scattering on Thermal Transport across Antiferromagnetic and Ferromagnetic Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32954)
*Zeyu Xiang,Bolin Liao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文结合第一性原理计算与自旋-晶格动力学，研究反铁磁NiO、MnO中声子-磁振子耦合及其对热输运的影响，并与同结构的铁磁EuO对比，发现自旋动力学降低声子热导率与寿命，且声子-磁振子能量尺度匹配和磁有序类型是决定散射强度的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子-磁振子耦合在铁磁体中已被广泛研究，但反铁磁体中其微观机制尚不清晰。作者希望理解反铁磁材料中声子-磁振子相互作用及其对热输运的影响机制。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算与自旋-晶格动力学相结合的方法，运用Green-Kubo公式与谱能量密度分析，对比研究反铁磁NiO、MnO与同结构铁磁EuO的声子-磁振子相互作用及热输运；并构建简化的相空间模型分析声子-磁振子散射通道。

Result: 自旋动力学降低声子热导率和声子寿命，其中MnO中效应最强。激发谱对比表明，自旋诱导的声子散射敏感地依赖于声子与磁振子能量尺度的相对大小。简化相空间模型表明，在声子与磁振子能量尺度相当时，反铁磁情形可承载比铁磁情形更大的散射相空间，即拥有更多运动学允许的散射通道。

Conclusion: 激发能量尺度和磁有序是决定磁材料中声子-磁振子散射的关键因素。

Abstract: Phonon--magnon coupling has been extensively studied in ferromagnets, whereas its microscopic mechanisms in antiferromagnets remain less understood. Here, we combine first-principles calculations with spin--lattice dynamics to investigate phonon--magnon interactions and their impact on thermal transport in antiferromagnetic NiO and MnO, which are put in contrast to the ferromagnetic EuO with an identical crystal structure. Green--Kubo and spectral energy density analyses show that spin dynamics reduce phonon thermal conductivity and lifetimes, with the strongest effects in MnO. Comparison of the excitation spectra indicates that spin-induced phonon scattering depends sensitively on the relative phonon and magnon energy scales, while the contrasting behavior of MnO and EuO further highlights the role of magnetic order. A simplified phase-space model further illustrates that, for comparable phonon and magnon energy scales, the antiferromagnetic case can support a larger phonon--magnon scattering phase space than the ferromagnetic case, suggesting a greater number of kinematically allowed scattering channels. These results identify excitation energy scales and magnetic order as key factors governing phonon--magnon scattering in magnetic materials.

</details>


### [101] [Piezo-Hall effect in $\mathrm{RbCr_2Se_2O}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33026)
*San-Dong Guo,Shi-Hao Zhang,Feng-Ren Fan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出并系统研究了压电霍尔效应（piezo-Hall effect）这一新概念：在原始状态下因磁空间群禁止净磁矩而无反常霍尔效应的材料，经施加应变改变磁空间群后可获得净磁矩，进而产生反常霍尔效应；并以 d 波交变磁性金属和具体材料 RbCr₂Se₂O 的第一性原理计算加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反常霍尔效应（AHE）对理解磁性材料中的自旋轨道耦合与磁输运至关重要，而应变能显著改变材料性质（压电效应为代表）。作者希望将 AHE 与应变这两个物理要素结合，探索磁空间群在应变调控下如何从禁止净磁矩的状态转变为允许净磁矩的状态，从而在原本无 AHE 的材料中诱导出 AHE，深化对应变与物性耦合的理解。

Method: 提出压电霍尔效应的概念框架：材料的磁空间群（MSG）在原始状态下禁止净磁矩、抑制 AHE，施加应变后材料转变为允许净磁矩的新的磁空间群，从而触发 AHE。机制涵盖应变诱导的磁电子态转变以及应变对磁化方向的调控。以 d 波交变磁性金属为模型进行详细分析，并对具体材料 RbCr₂Se₂O 开展第一性原理计算予以验证。

Result: 在 d 波交变磁性金属和 RbCr₂Se₂O 中验证了压电霍尔效应。显著结果是：沿 x 方向和 y 方向施加相同大小的单轴应变时，反常霍尔电导率符号相反、大小相等。进一步提出压电磁光效应；若以电场调制替代应变调控，可相应提出电霍尔效应和电磁光效应；甚至温度也可作为外场引入温度霍尔效应。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了压电霍尔效应这一物理过程，并通过第一性原理计算在 RbCr₂Se₂O 中验证其可行性，揭示了应变-磁空间群-反常霍尔效应之间的内在联系，同时将概念推广到电霍尔、磁光、温度霍尔等效应，推动了对应变与物性耦合机制的认识。

Abstract: The anomalous Hall effect (AHE) is of fundamental importance for understanding spin-orbit coupling and magnetic transport in magnetic materials. Meanwhile, strain can significantly modify material properties, and the piezoelectric effect stands as a well-known representative. The piezo-Hall effect combines the two physical ingredients of AHE and strain. Specifically, the piezo-Hall effect describes a physical process in which the magnetic space group (MSG) of a magnetic material prohibits a net magnetic moment and suppresses the AHE in the pristine state but transforms into a new MSG upon strain application that permits a net magnetic moment, thereby enabling the emergence of the AHE. The underlying mechanisms cover strain-induced magnetic electronic state transition and strain tuning of magnetization direction. We take the $d$-wave altermagnetic metal as an example to analyze the piezo-Hall effect in detail, with first-principles calculations carried out on the specific material $\mathrm{RbCr_2Se_2O}$ to verify this scenario. A notable result is that under identical uniaxial strain applied along the $x$ and $y$ directions, the anomalous Hall conductivity exhibits opposite signs but equal magnitudes. Similarly, the piezo-magneto-optical effect can also be proposed, and if strain tuning is replaced by electric-field modulation, the electro-Hall effect and electro-magneto-optical effect can be also put forward accordingly. Even temperature can serve as an external field to introduce the temperature Hall effect. Our work advances the understanding of the coupling between strain and physical properties.

</details>


### [102] [Pressure-induced s,p-d electron redistribution accompanies structural transformation in amorphous Zr-Cu alloy under compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35380)
*P. Dziegielewski,J. Antonowicz,K. Georgarakis,O. Lord,M. Amboage,D. Daisenberger,L. Clough,T. Irifune,T. Shinmei*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在高压下，非晶Zr67Cu33合金中的Zr原子发生s、p电子向d态转移的电荷重排，Cu受影响较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端压缩可重组原子价电子态，在某些条件下将电荷从空间延展的轨道驱动到更紧凑的轨道。本文旨在研究非晶Zr67Cu33金属玻璃中这一效应，并将其与此前报道的压缩非晶合金异常结构演化联系起来。

Method: 结合高达69 GPa的高压X射线吸收精细结构（XAFS）测量、分子动力学模拟以及密度泛函理论计算。通过Zr K边XANES谱观察前边峰强度变化，分子动力学模拟再现EXAFS谱，并计算Mulliken布居与态密度。

Result: Zr K边XANES谱显示前边峰强度显著增强且压力依赖性改变，而Cu响应明显较弱；Mulliken布居分析显示Zr的s、p态随压力耗减，d态占据增加；态密度表明压缩下p-d杂化增强。这些结果为Zr中压力诱导的s,p-d电子重排提供了实验与计算证据。

Conclusion: 非晶Zr67Cu33在压力下Zr发生s,p→d电荷转移，该效应与压缩非晶合金的异常结构演化相关联。

Abstract: Extreme compression can reorganize atomic valence states, in some regimes driving charge from spatially extended orbitals into more compact ones. We investigate this effect in amorphous Zr67Cu33 metallic glass by combining high-pressure X-ray absorption fine structure measurements up to 69 GPa with molecular dynamics simulations and density functional theory calculations. The Zr K-edge XANES spectra show a pronounced increase in the pre-edge intensity and a change in its pressure dependence, whereas the Cu response is markedly weaker. Molecular-dynamics simulations reproduce the measured EXAFS spectra and provide reliable input for the electronic-structure calculations. The calculated Mulliken populations reveal pressure-induced depletion of Zr s and p-states, accompanied by an increase of the d-state population, while the density of states indicates enhanced p-d hybridization under compression. These results provide experimental and computational evidence for pressure-induced s,p-d electronic redistribution in zirconium and link this effect to the previously reported anomalous structural evolution of the compressed amorphous alloy.

</details>


### [103] [Unifying Magnetic, Electrical, and Thermoelectric Responses in a Non-Collinear Antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33046)
*André José,Adrielson Dias,Carlos Eduardo,Giovanna Celly,Luiza Paffer,José Araújo,José Laurentino,Monalisa Cavalcante,José Holanda*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究IrMn₃(非共线反铁磁体)/Py(坡莫合金)异质结构中的交换偏置效应，发现纵向MOKE、AMR和ANE三种独立测量方法均给出约50 Oe的单向场偏移及一致的余弦角度依赖，揭示非共线反铁磁体对相邻铁磁体施加的界面磁对称性会一致地体现于磁化反转、电荷输运和热驱动电压产生中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非共线反铁磁体因其复杂自旋构型和界面对称性，为调控磁、电和热电响应提供了非常规途径。然而，交换偏置效应传统上主要局限于共线反铁磁体系，其在非共线体系中对多个物理观测量的协同体现尚未被系统验证。本文旨在揭示非共线反铁磁体所限定的界面磁对称性如何一致地映射到相邻铁磁体的多种响应中，从而扩展交换偏置的理解范畴。

Method: 构建了10 nm IrMn₃与5 nm Py耦合的异质结构，采用三种独立的测量手段——纵向磁光克尔效应（MOKE）、各向异性磁电阻（AMR）和反常能斯特效应（ANE）——分别对磁化反转、电荷输运和热驱动电压进行表征，并比较三者得到的单向场偏移值和角度依赖关系。

Result: 三种测量方法独立且一致地揭示了约50 Oe的单向场偏移，并呈现一致的余弦角度依赖关系。该结果表明非共线反铁磁体施加的界面磁对称性同时体现在磁化反转、电荷输运和热驱动电压产生中，交换偏置印记并非局限于单一实验观测量。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了IrMn₃/Py的相干多功能响应，将交换偏置的常规理解从共线反铁磁体系扩展到非共线反铁磁体系，并展示了非共线反铁磁体作为耦合磁、电、热电功能的平台，在自旋电子学和自旋卡路里电子学器件中的潜力。

Abstract: Non-collinear antiferromagnets offer unconventional routes for controlling magnetic, electrical, and thermoelectric responses through their complex spin configurations and interfacial symmetry. Here, we detect exceptional interfacial properties arising from a non-collinear antiferromagnet by demonstrating a robust and quantitatively consistent exchange-bias response in an IrMn$_3$/Py heterostructure. The system consists of a 10 nm IrMn$_3$ layer coupled to a 5 nm permalloy (Py) film. Longitudinal magneto-optical Kerr effect (MOKE), anisotropic magnetoresistance (AMR), and anomalous Nernst effect (ANE) measurements independently reveal the same unidirectional field shift of approximately 50 Oe, together with a consistent cosine angular dependence. The simultaneous observation of these signatures establishes a direct correspondence between the interfacial magnetic symmetry imposed by the non-collinear antiferromagnet and the responses of the adjacent ferromagnet. This symmetry is manifested consistently in magnetization reversal, charge transport, and thermally driven voltage generation, demonstrating that the exchange-bias imprint is not restricted to a single experimental observable. Our results reveal a coherent multifunctional response of IrMn$_3$/Py and extend the conventional understanding of exchange bias beyond collinear antiferromagnetic systems. More broadly, they demonstrate the potential of non-collinear antiferromagnets as platforms for coupling magnetic, electrical, and thermoelectric functionalities in spintronic and spin-caloritronic devices.

</details>


### [104] [Unveiling Lattice Dynamics and a Hidden Structural Transition in the 2D Ferromagnet AgVP$_2$Se$_6$ via Raman Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33116)
*Thi Hai Yen Pham,Po-hao Chang,Aidan C. Malloy,Seng Huat Lee,Zhiqiang Mao,Maria F. Munoz,Riccardo Torsi,Angela R. Hight Walker,Igor Mazin,Patrick M. Vora*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文对二维范德华铁磁材料 AgVP₂Se₆ 单晶进行了偏振分辨、温度依赖的拉曼与磁拉曼光谱研究，并结合 DFT 计算，探究其晶格动力学、自旋-声子耦合及电子结构，为自旋电子学应用提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 AgVP₂Se₆ 的拉曼研究均在远高于居里温度（T_C）下进行，无法研究自旋-声子耦合；且其输运激活能隙（约0.325 eV）与光吸收边（约2.14 eV）存在巨大差异，磁性质与硫化物 AgVP₂S₆ 也显著不同，这些都有待厘清。

Method: 采用化学气相输运（CVT）和助熔剂法生长 AgVP₂Se₆ 单晶，进行偏振分辨、温度依赖的拉曼光谱与磁拉曼光谱测量（跨越 FM 相变），并结合 DFT 计算声子、光学与磁性质。

Result: 低温拉曼谱解析出多达30个峰，角分辨测量完成对称性归属；CVT 与助熔剂晶体光谱差异源于不同层间堆垛畴；温变与场变光谱在 T_C 附近几乎不变，表明自旋-声子耦合较弱；约100 K 处明显的光谱变化提示可能的相变；DFT 计算将输运激活能隙归因于光学暗、但热可及的 V-d 上哈伯德带跃迁，并解释了硫化物与硒化物磁性质差异。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了 AgVP₂Se₆ 的晶格动力学特征与弱自旋-声子耦合，澄清了输运与光学能隙矛盾及磁性质差异的机理，为未来自旋电子学应用提供关键指导。

Abstract: Transition-metal chalcophosphates (TMCs) are two-dimensional (2D) van der Waals materials supporting a broad range of electronic and magnetic properties. Among quaternary TMCs, AgVP$_2$Se$_6$ is a rare ferromagnet (FM) with a triangular V sublattice, in contrast to the antiferromagnetic (AFM) zigzag chains of the sulfide AgVP$_2$S$_6$. Prior Raman studies of AgVP$_2$Se$_6$ have been performed far above the Curie temperature ($T_C$), preventing studies of spin-phonon coupling. Here, we report polarization-resolved, temperature-dependent Raman and magneto-Raman spectroscopy of AgVP$_2$Se$_6$ single crystals grown by chemical vapor transport (CVT) and flux methods, across the FM transition, complemented by density functional theory (DFT) calculations of phonon, optical and magnetic properties. Cryogenic Raman spectra resolve up to thirty peaks, while angle-resolved measurements enable their symmetry assignment. Spectral differences between CVT- and flux-grown crystals are traced back to the presence of different interlayer stacking domains in these samples. Temperature- and field-dependent Raman spectra remain largely unchanged across $T_C$, suggesting weak spin-phonon coupling. However, pronounced spectral alterations near 100 K suggest a potential structural phase transition. Our DFT calculations further reconcile the large discrepancy between the transport activation gap ($\approx$ 0.325 eV) and optical absorption edge ($\approx$ 2.14 eV), attributing the former to a transition to the V-d upper Hubbard band that is optically dark but thermally accessible. We also suggest a theoretical explanation of the qualitative difference (reproduced by DFT calculations) between the magnetic properties of the sulfide and selenide. These results provide key information regarding the lattice dynamics of AgVP$_2$Se$_6$ and will guide future applications in spin-based electronics.

</details>


### [105] [Symmetry-guided Design Principles for Spin Splitting and Hall Transport in Orthorhombic Altermagnetic Perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33130)
*Rasmita Kumari,Bishal Das,Aftab Alam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文建立了正交钙钛矿（Pbnm）中共线反铁磁体的统一对称性框架，揭示了磁序不可约表示如何唯一决定交变磁自旋劈裂的动量平面、低能有效哈密顿量、弱铁磁倾斜取向及反常霍尔电导张量，并通过8种实验氧化物验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体（altermagnets）在无自旋轨道耦合（SOC）下即可产生动量依赖的自旋劈裂能带，但缺乏一个统一的对称性理论框架来预测其自旋劈裂、磁输运及磁序之间的系统性联系，尤其对于结构同构的正交Pbnm钙钛矿体系。

Method: 结合自旋群（spin group）与磁群（magnetic group）理论及第一性原理计算，分析正交Pbnm钙钛矿中共线反铁磁序的不可约表示，推导其决定的动量空间自旋劈裂平面、低能有效哈密顿量形式、SOC诱导的弱铁磁倾斜方向，以及允许的反常霍尔电导张量分量，并在8种实验已实现的绝缘与金属性正交钙钛矿氧化物中进行验证。

Result: 磁序的不可约表示唯一确定动量空间支持交变磁自旋劈裂的平面、低能有效哈密顿量形式、弱铁磁倾斜取向及AHC张量分量。预测在8种实验氧化物中得到验证：LaTiO3和CaCrO3分别表现出约38和205 S/cm的可观反常霍尔电导，尽管其净磁化近乎为零。SOC打开受对称性保护的交变磁能带交叉点间隙，产生大贝里曲率，而霍耳响应根源于非相对论自旋劈裂。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了预测性的对称性框架，用于发现和设计具有可调自旋依赖电子与输运性质的交变磁材料，确立了从磁序不可约表示出发预测交变磁与反常霍尔效应的统一范式。

Abstract: Magnetic symmetry can generate momentum-dependent spin-split electronic bands even in the absence of spin-orbit coupling (SOC), giving rise to the recently discovered class of altermagnets. Here, we establish a unified symmetry framework for collinear antiferromagnets in orthorhombic Pbnm perovskites by combining spin and magnetic group theory with first-principles calculations. We show that the irreducible representation of the magnetic order uniquely determines the momentum-space planes supporting altermagnetic spin splitting, the form of the effective low-energy Hamiltonian, the orientation of SOC-induced weak ferromagnetic canting, and the allowed anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) tensor components. These predictions are validated in eight experimentally realized orthorhombic perovskite oxides spanning both insulating and metallic regimes. In particular, LaTiO3 and CaCrO3 exhibit sizable anomalous Hall conductivities of approximately 38 and 205 S/cm, respectively, despite nearly vanishing net magnetization. We further show that SOC gaps symmetry-protected altermagnetic band crossings, generating large Berry curvature, while the Hall response is fundamentally rooted in the underlying non-relativistic spin splitting. Our work establishes a predictive symmetry-based framework for discovering and engineering altermagnetic materials with tunable spin-dependent electronic and transport properties.

</details>


### [106] [Low-temperature thermal expansion of the $α$ and $ω$ phases of titanium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33302)
*Norimasa Nishiyama,Atsushi Togo,Yoshitaka Matsushita*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过X射线衍射（约5–300 K）与第一性原理计算，研究了钛的α相与高压多型ω相的低温热膨胀行为，发现两相体热膨胀相近但轴向响应差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钛的α相与ω相（高压多型）在低温下的各向异性热膨胀行为尚未被精确表征，尤其是此前单晶长度测量可能高估了α相c轴的负热膨胀，需要结合实验与理论加以厘清。

Method: 在7.7 GPa、500 ℃下合成单相块体多晶ω-Ti并降压回收至常压；利用X射线衍射在约5–300 K测量两相的点阵参数；结合第一性原理计算模拟热膨胀，并用Debye-Grüneisen分析提取德拜温度与有效Grüneisen参数。

Result: 两相体热膨胀非常相似，但轴向行为差异明显：α-Ti的c轴热膨胀系数在约50 K以下变为负值，20 K时达到最小值-0.71×10⁻⁶ K⁻¹；实验与理论均表明该c轴负热膨胀远小于此前单晶长度测量报道值。ω-Ti沿a、c轴近各向同性膨胀，c/a比几乎不随温度变化且接近理想bcc衍生值；α-Ti的c/a比随温度升高而减小。Debye-Grüneisen分析得到α-Ti与ω-Ti的德拜温度分别为427±5 K与409±12 K，有效Grüneisen参数分别为1.33±0.04与1.23±0.04。第一性原理计算复现了两相的体热膨胀，并捕捉到轴向响应总体趋势。

Conclusion: α-Ti与ω-Ti体热膨胀相近但轴向各向异性截然不同：α-Ti呈现显著的低温c轴负热膨胀（且弱于以往报道），ω-Ti则近似各向同性膨胀。第一性原理计算与实验一致，为理解钛两种相的热力学行为提供了定量依据。

Abstract: We investigated the low-temperature thermal expansion of the $α$ phase and the $ω$ phase, a high-pressure polymorph of titanium, by X-ray diffraction measurements from approximately 5 to 300 K, together with first-principles calculations. Single-phase bulk polycrystalline $ω$-Ti was synthesized at 7.7 GPa and 500 $^\circ$C and recovered to ambient pressure. Although the two phases exhibit very similar volumetric thermal expansion, their axial responses are markedly different. In $α$-Ti, the $c$-axis thermal expansion coefficient becomes negative below approximately 50 K and reaches a minimum of $-0.71 \times 10^{-6}$ K$^{-1}$ at 20 K. Both experiment and theory indicate substantially smaller $c$-axis negative thermal expansion than previously reported from single-crystal length measurements. In contrast, $ω$-Ti expands nearly isotropically along the $a$ and $c$ axes, and its $c/a$ ratio remains nearly temperature independent and close to the ideal bcc-derived value. The $c$/$a$ ratio of $α$-Ti decreases with increasing temperature. Debye-Grüneisen analysis yielded Debye temperatures of $427 \pm 5$ K and $409 \pm 12$ K and effective Grüneisen parameters of $1.33 \pm 0.04$ and $1.23 \pm 0.04$ for $α$-Ti and $ω$-Ti, respectively. The first-principles calculations reproduce the volumetric thermal expansion of both phases and capture the overall trends of their axial responses.

</details>


### [107] [Let CSP Be Your ANCHOR: Adaptive Crystal Search over Frozen Structure Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33407)
*Emma Lei Hovmand,Jonas Elsborg,Melih Kandemir,Arghya Bhowmik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出 ANCHOR，将晶体结构预测（CSP）模型作为冻结的物理先验，仅对成分搜索策略进行多目标奖励（GRPO）训练，并用连续自适应新颖性（CAN）替代离散去重，显著提升新材料发现率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: De novo 晶体生成（DNG）模型把"在成分空间中搜索哪里"和"如何生成结构"耦合在同一组权重中，导致发现效率受限。作者认为发现应解耦：CSP 模型是应通过似然训练改进的物理先验，而新颖性等奖励应只作用于成分空间的搜索策略。

Method: 引入 ANCHOR——一个围绕冻结 CSP 模型的 GRPO 成分策略，使用多目标奖励训练；并引入 CAN（连续自适应新颖性），一种针对已知结构和不断增长的发现历史的分级新颖性评分。用冻结 CSP 模型作为固定"标尺"，在单一评估器下测试自适应应作用于何处。

Result: 在相同 CSP 骨干上用 ANCHOR 策略替换 DNG 成分，99.9% 成分唯一性下 MSUN 从 11.4% 升至 47.6%，SUN 从 1.1% 升至 22.1%。直接对 DNG 模型微调相同奖励只会移动成分边际而无法提高 hull 上比例。KL 正则化的 DNG 微调只能以有界因子重加权已有化学，而无正则化的 DNG 微调会向已知或更不稳定化学漂移。仅将稳定性奖励引入 ANCHOR 的 CSP 骨干会使 SUN 相对冻结骨干约减半，而基于搜索中结构的似然训练可改进 CSP 骨干。在 MatterGen 评估流程下，ANCHOR 将 SOTA MSUN 从 29.2% 提至 41.3%，无需重训即可迁移到两个额外 CSP 骨干，蒸馏进 Crystalite-CSP 后达 47.1%。

Conclusion: 解耦成分搜索与结构生成优于端到端 DNG 微调；奖励应作用于搜索策略，而 CSP 骨干通过似然训练改进。ANCHOR 跨骨干可迁移、可蒸馏，但其 hull 上率最终取决于底层用于优化的势能。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation (DNG) models decide where to search in composition space and how to generate structures with one set of weights. We argue that discovery is better served by separating the two. A crystal structure prediction (CSP) model is a physical prior that should be improved by likelihood training, while rewards, including novelty measured against the search's own history, should act on a search over compositions. We introduce ANCHOR, a GRPO composition policy trained with multi-objective rewards around a frozen CSP model, and continuous adaptive novelty (CAN), a graded novelty score against known structures and a growing discovery history. Using the frozen CSP model as a fixed ruler under one evaluator, we test where adaptation should act. Replacing DNG compositions with ANCHOR's policy on the same CSP backbone raises MSUN from 11.4% to 47.6% and SUN from 1.1% to 22.1% at 99.9% formula uniqueness. Fine-tuning DNG models directly on the same rewards instead moves their composition marginal without raising their on-hull fraction. We show that KL-regularized fine-tuning of a DNG model can only reweight chemistry the pretrained model already supports by a bounded factor, while unregularized DNG fine-tunes move toward known or less stable chemistry. Even a stability-only reward routed into ANCHOR's CSP backbone roughly halves SUN relative to the frozen backbone, whereas likelihood training on structures found during search can improve a CSP backbone. Under MatterGen's evaluation pipeline, ANCHOR raises state-of-the-art MSUN from 29.2% to 41.3%, transfers without retraining to two further CSP backbones, and reaches 47.1% after distillation into Crystalite-CSP. As with any model optimised against a potential, its on-hull rate depends on that potential.

</details>


### [108] [Temperature Periodic Modulation Doubles the Power Output of Thermoelectric Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33425)
*Dario Narducci,Antonio Mazzacua,Federico Giulio*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过周期性调制热源温度来显著提升热电能量收集输出功率的方法。研究表明，采用不同于正弦调制的周期调制方式，可使输出功率较稳态时提升高达100%，且该结论在考虑材料物性温度依赖的实际情况（超出常物性近似）下仍然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热电转换效率长期以来偏低，尽管过去二十年对材料性能投入了大量改进努力。作者希望探索一种此前未充分开发的动态热通量转换途径，以突破热电装置效率限制。

Method: 通过对热源温度施加周期性（时间相关）调制，而非传统的稳态工作方式。作者发现采用某种不同于正弦调制的周期调制波形，能超越此前正弦调制带来的功率增益；并将分析从常物性近似扩展到考虑所有唯象系数温度依赖的真实材料情形。

Result: 与稳态情况相比，优化后的周期调制可使输出功率提升高达100%（相比之下，此前正弦调制最多提升50%）。此外，该功率增益在计入材料物性温度依赖后依然稳健，适用于任意真实材料。

Conclusion: 这一显著的功率输出提升为突破热电装置效率极限提供了一条新颖且可立即应用的途径，有望有效回收废热并为离网设备供电。

Abstract: Thermoelectric heat harvesting provides in principle a flexible, reliable way to convert heat into electric energy, thus recovering waste heat and powering off-net devices. However, thermoelectricity conversion efficiency is still low, despite the major efforts deployed over the last two decades to improve materials performances. A largely unexplored opportunity to increase harvesting efficiency is offered by converting heat flux dynamically. It was shown [D. Narducci et al., Mater. Today Phys. 54, 101713 (2025)] that when the temperature of the heat source is sinusoidally modulated, this leads to an increase of the maximum power by up to 50 %. In this paper we show how this approach can be further and significantly enhanced. We found that sinusoidal modulation can be outperformed by a different periodic modulation, leading to an increase of the power output by up to 100 % compared to the stationary case. Furthermore, we extended our analyses beyond the constant-property approximation, showing that the power output enhancement is robust, applying to any real material where the temperature dependence of all phenomenological coefficients is accounted for. This major power output enhancement offers a novel, immediately applicable pathway to overcome the efficiency limits of thermoelectric devices.

</details>


### [109] [Toward catalyst-mediated growth of diamond and cubic boron nitride wires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33478)
*Rodney S. Ruoff*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可复现的金刚石与立方氮化硼纳米/微米线催化剂辅助连续生长方法，通过VLS/VSS机制及多种接触方式切换策略，兼顾四面体键合与原子持续供应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前尚无可持续催化剂介导生长金刚石和立方氮化硼线的可复现方法；而已有催化剂辅助生长已成功用于碳和氮化硼纳米管及多种元素、化合物与合金纳米线。金刚石和cBN线可兼具高比刚度、高强度、导热与电绝缘性能，具有重要应用价值。

Method: 提出气-液-固（VLS）与气-固-固（VSS）生长路线，在首个金刚石或cBN晶体段形成与其后续生长之间切换温度、进料、催化剂或起始接触方式。具体策略包括：从碳化物或富硼起始线切换、在既有晶体的选定晶面上沉积催化剂、以及利用局部固态原料在初始生长阶段向催化剂供应原子。同时结合历史上的金刚石晶须报道，重新测试金刚石-金属接触。

Result: 文章为提案性质，尚无具体实验数据；提出可检验的问题——上述金刚石-金属接触能否在维持原子持续供给的同时促进四面体键合，目标产物涵盖单晶与多晶，直径从纳米至微米及以上。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的VLS/VSS策略和接触切换，有望建立可复现的催化剂辅助金刚石与立方氮化硼线生长方法，获得兼具力学、热学与电学优异性能的一维材料。

Abstract: A reproducible method for sustained catalyst-mediated growth of diamond and cubic boron nitride (cBN) wires has yet to be established. Catalyst-assisted growth has produced carbon and boron nitride nanotubes and a wide range of elemental, compound and alloy nanowires. Diamond and cBN wires could combine high specific stiffness and strength with heat conduction and electrical insulation. I propose vapor-liquid-solid (VLS) and vapor-solid-solid (VSS) routes in which temperature, feed, catalyst or starting contact changes between formation of the first diamond or cBN segment and its continued growth. The approaches include switching from carbide or boron-rich starter wires, depositing catalyst on selected facets of existing crystals, and using local solid feedstock to supply the catalyst during initial growth. Historical diamond-whisker reports motivate renewed tests of diamond-metal contacts. The proposed experiments ask whether these contacts can favor tetrahedral bonding while maintaining a supply of atoms to the growing wire. Both single-crystal and polycrystalline products, from nanometer to micrometer and larger diameters, are of interest.

</details>


### [110] [Computational symmetry hierarchy of time-reversal-even and -odd spin Hall conductivity tensors in altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33631)
*Dameul Jeong,Seoung-Hun Kang,Young-Kyun Kwon*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出将交错磁体（altermagnet）的自旋霍尔电导率分解为时间反演偶通道与时间反演奇通道，结合对称性分析与第一性原理计算，建立27分量SHC张量的线性约束框架，实现对磁晶体的高通量预筛选，并生成62种共线反铁磁体的磁点群张量图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁晶体的自旋霍尔电导率（SHC）全张量预测困难，因为晶体学对称性与磁对称性对响应不同分量的约束方式并不一致，传统方法无法系统区分哪些张量分量在对称性上被允许。

Method: 将交错磁体的SHC分解为时间反演偶（T-even）与时间反演奇（T-odd）两个通道，对六个代表性化合物（覆盖不同晶体学类）进行对称性分析与第一性原理计算；把晶体学与磁对称性操作转化为27分量SHC张量的线性约束，并通过动量分辨分析将偶响应溯源至自旋轨道耦合驱动的能带回避交叉、将奇响应溯源至近费米能级各向异性自旋流响应织构；进一步扩展分析至62种自旋劈裂共线反铁磁体，构建磁点群张量图谱。

Result: 偶通道遵循与Berry曲率费米海起源一致的晶体学选择定则；奇通道由磁对称性决定，可被空间部分源自螺旋轴或滑移面的反幺正操作重塑。动量分辨分析将偶响应与自旋轨道耦合驱动的回避交叉关联、将奇响应与近费米能级的各向异性自旋流响应织构关联。62种材料图谱揭示设计极限，如CuF2的完整13/14张量划分和MnSe的单参数时间奇张量压缩。

Conclusion: 该框架通过在密集第一性原理输运计算之前将对称性操作转化为SHC张量的线性约束，提供了一条计算预筛选途径，用于识别对称性允许的自旋霍尔通道，并揭示交错磁体SHC张量的设计极限。

Abstract: Predicting the full spin Hall conductivity tensor of a magnetic crystal is difficult because crystallographic and magnetic symmetries do not constrain all parts of the response in the same way. Here, we separate the spin Hall conductivity of altermagnets into time-reversal-even and time-reversal-odd channels and combine symmetry analysis with first-principles calculations for six representative compounds spanning distinct crystallographic classes. The even channel follows crystallographic selection rules consistent with its Berry-curvature Fermi-sea origin. The odd channel is set by magnetic symmetry and can be reshaped by antiunitary operations whose spatial parts originate from screw or glide symmetries. Momentum-resolved analysis further links the even response to spin-orbit-driven avoided crossings and the odd response to anisotropic near-Fermi-level spin-current response textures. By converting crystallographic and magnetic symmetry operations into linear constraints on the full 27-component SHC tensor, this framework provides a computational prescreening route for identifying symmetry-allowed spin Hall channels before dense first-principles transport calculations. Extending the same analysis to 62 spin-split collinear antiferromagnets yields a magnetic-point-group tensor atlas, identifying design limits such as the complete 13/14tensor partition in CuF2 and the one-parameter time-odd tensor compression in MnSe.

</details>


### [111] [Placement-driven reversal of preferred spin alignment in monolayer hBN vacancy pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33714)
*Daniel Hashemi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用密度泛函理论（DFT）研究单层六方氮化硼（hBN）中两个带负电硼空位的自旋耦合，发现空位间距和相对位置会决定其集体自旋排列偏好：最近邻配置（4.3 Å）倾向平行排列，而相距7.5 Å的配置倾向反平行排列，为原子级薄材料中耦合自旋中心提供了微观理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带负电的硼空位是hBN中可光学寻址的自旋中心，但将两个空位靠近会引入孤立缺陷图像中不存在的电子相互作用。此前缺乏对空位间距与排列方式如何影响其自旋耦合的原子尺度微观理解。

Method: 在总电荷q=-2条件下采用密度泛函理论，对单层hBN中七种双空位构型进行计算。使用半局域近似比较能量差，通过改变空位相对位置（移动一个晶格矢量）观察自旋偏好反转，并采用数值控制（变化小于1 meV）和杂化泛函计算验证结论的稳健性；同时分析局域畸变、空位态杂化及自旋密度分布，考察偏离双线性海森堡形式的角能量。

Result: 七种构型中，最近邻空位对（4.3 Å）在半局域近似下以27.6 meV偏好平行自旋排列；移动一个晶格矢量后偏好反转，7.5 Å间距空位对以50.7 meV偏好反平行排列。数值控制对这两个参考劈裂的改变小于1 meV；杂化泛函计算保持其符号但显著改变幅度。7.5 Å处的反平行偏好源于空位态沿中间原子链杂化的变化；最近邻空位对则表现出接触局域化的自旋密度反差及偏离双线性海森堡形式的受限角能量。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了依赖空位放置方式的自旋相互作用区间，为在原子级薄材料中研究和构建耦合自旋中心提供了微观基础。

Abstract: The negatively charged boron vacancy is an optically addressable spin centre in hexagonal boron nitride (hBN), but bringing two vacancies together introduces electronic interactions absent from the isolated-defect picture. Here we use density-functional theory at total charge q = -2 to show that atomic-scale placement reverses the preferred collinear spin alignment in monolayer hBN. Among seven configurations, the closest pair, separated by 4.3 angstrom, favours parallel alignment by 27.6 meV within the semilocal approximation. Moving one vacancy by a single lattice vector reverses the preference, and a 7.5 angstrom pair favours antiparallel alignment by 50.7 meV. Numerical controls change these two reference splittings by less than 1 meV; hybrid-functional calculations retain their signs while substantially changing their magnitudes. Local distortions connect the antiparallel preference at 7.5 angstrom to changes in vacancy-state hybridization along an intervening atomic chain. The closest pair instead exhibits a contact-localized spin-density contrast and constrained angular energies that depart from the bilinear Heisenberg form. These results identify a placement-sensitive regime of interacting defects and provide a microscopic foundation for investigating coupled spin centres in atomically thin materials.

</details>


### [112] [Benchmark of First-Principles Titanium K-Edge X-Ray Absorption Spectral Simulations on Titanium-containing Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33776)
*Chuntian Cao,Joshua J. Kas,Karol Dyro,Bruce Ravel,John Vinson,Deyu Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过对比九种常见钛化合物的Ti K-edge XANES实验数据，系统基准测试了第一性原理光谱模拟方法，揭示了四极激发、热无序和多体shake-up效应对光谱精度的关键贡献，并建立了生成高保真Ti K-edge XANES数据库的可推广工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAS是一种强大的元素特异性探针，但其定量分析困难，需要精确的第一性原理光谱模拟。本研究旨在系统基准测试和识别影响Ti K-edge XANES模拟精度的关键物理效应，以提升光谱模拟的可靠性。

Method: 作者对九种常见钛化合物进行了Ti K-edge XANES的第一性原理模拟，并与实验数据对比。系统考察了四极激发、热无序、多体shake-up效应，并结合芯空穴末态的能带理论处理，通过相似度评分评估模拟与实验的一致性。针对BaTiO3肩峰的缺失，进一步分析了电子结构理论（半局域密度泛函理论的局限）和缺陷（如氧空位）的影响。

Result: 四极激发和热无序对于准确再现前边特征至关重要；多体shake-up效应显著影响主边和后边区域的光谱形状。综合这些效应后，大多数体系的模拟光谱与实验数据达到高度一致（高相似度分数）。但BaTiO3在4980 eV的实验肩峰在模拟中基本缺失，分析表明需要比半局域密度泛函理论更精确的电子结构理论来捕捉Ba 4f轨道的关联效应，且氧空位等缺陷可能也是肩峰的来源。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了生成高保真Ti K-edge XANES数据库的稳健工作流程，该框架可推广到更广泛的材料体系。同时指出，对于含有强关联轨道（如Ba 4f）或缺陷的体系，仍需更高级的电子结构理论来进一步提升模拟精度。

Abstract: X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) is a powerful, element-specific probe for investigating the local structural and electronic properties of materials. However, quantitative analysis remains challenging, necessitating accurate first-principles spectral simulations. In this study, we benchmark first-principles simulations of Ti K-edge X-ray absorption near-edge structure (XANES) against experimental data for nine common titanium compounds. We systematically investigate key physical effects, including quadrupole excitations, thermal disorder, and many-body shake-up. Our results demonstrate that quadrupole excitations and thermal disorder are essential for capturing accurate pre-edge features, while many-body shake-up effects significantly influence the spectral shape of the main- and post-edge regions. By incorporating these effects alongside a band-theory treatment of the core-hole final state, our simulated spectra achieve excellent agreement with experimental data for most of the systems, as evidenced by high similarity scores. The shoulder peak in BaTiO$_3$ at 4980 eV in the experiment is largely missing in simulation. Further analysis shows that more accurate electronic structure theory than semi-local density functional theory is required to capture the correlation effects of the Ba $4f$ orbitals and that defects, such as oxygen vacancies, may also contribute to the shoulder. Beyond tackling specific titanium material systems, this work establishes a robust workflow for generating high-fidelity Ti K-edge XANES databases for titanium compounds, providing a framework that can be generalized to a broad range of materials.

</details>


### [113] [Democratizing Atomistic Simulation Workflows for the AI Era with the Quantum Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33823)
*Andrew S. Rosen,Naisargi Goyal,Brad Ayers,Vineet Bansal,Julia H. Baratta,Samuel M. Blau,Yuan Chiang,Sihoon Choi,Orion Archer Cohen,Blake Dallmann,Tom Demeyere,Will Engler,Yue-Wen Fang,Isabella Furrick,Eliu Huerta,Honghui Kim,Hironori Kondo,Anup Kumar,Jaehong Kwon,Osman Mamun,Charles B. Musgrave,Hananeh Oliaei,Aryan Saha,Davide Sarpa,Benjamin X. Shi,Yuliang Shi,Xing Wang,Robert B. Wexler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: QuAcc 是一个开源原子模拟工作流库，以量子力学计算为重点，通过将科学逻辑与工作流引擎解耦，支持多种计算方法和引擎，并统一评估基础机器学习原子间势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习和人工智能在原子模拟领域日益重要，研究者需要在多种方法（第一性原理、半经验/紧束缚、经典势、基础机器学习原子间势）之间灵活切换、快速构建工作流，同时避免在评估基础机器学习原子间势时因参考计算不一致而产生的“方法论漂移”。现有工具往往将科学逻辑与工作流引擎耦合，开发与贡献新工作流门槛较高。

Method: 提出 QuAcc 工作流库，其核心设计是将领域科学逻辑与工作流引擎相分离：工作流以普通 Python 函数编写，无需修改源码即可在多个受支持的工作流引擎上执行。提供覆盖第一性原理电子结构、半经验与紧束缚、经典势及基础机器学习原子间势的预定义工作流配方，并构建统一平台生成与目标模型一致的从头算参考计算。

Result: QuAcc 成功实现科学逻辑与工作流引擎的解耦，用户可在多个引擎间运行同一工作流而无需改动底层代码，降低了开发与贡献新工作流的门槛；同时提供统一的从头算参考计算生成平台，有效缓解了评估基础机器学习原子间势时的方法论漂移问题。

Conclusion: QuAcc 为当前机器学习和人工智能时代的原子模拟工作流提供了一个灵活、易用、可扩展的开源框架，是衔接多种计算方法与模型评估的重要基础设施。

Abstract: We present the Quantum Accelerator (QuAcc), an open-source workflow library for atomistic simulations with an emphasis on quantum-mechanical calculations. QuAcc provides predefined workflow recipes spanning first-principles electronic-structure methods, semiempirical and tight-binding approaches, classical potentials, and foundation machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs). A central design feature of QuAcc is its separation of domain-specific scientific logic from the workflow engine used to orchestrate and execute calculations. Workflows are written as ordinary Python functions and can be executed with multiple supported workflow engines without modifying the underlying source code, lowering the barrier to developing and contributing new workflows. QuAcc also streamlines the evaluation of foundation MLIPs by providing a unified platform for generating ab initio reference calculations consistent with the model of interest, mitigating methodological drift when assessing model performance. Together, these features make QuAcc a flexible and accessible framework for atomistic simulation workflows that have become central to the current era of machine learning and artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [114] [Correlation Between Dopant Atom Evaporation Field and Measured Site Preference in Atom Probe Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34008)
*Kasper A. Hunnestad,Constantinos Hatzoglou,Frida H. Danmo,Zewu Yan,Edith Bourret,Francois Vurpillot,Antonius T. J. van Helvoort,Sverre Selbach,Dennis Meier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文以Zr掺杂ErMnO3为模型体系，首次揭示了原子探针层析(APT)测量中掺杂原子位置与场蒸发条件的关系，发现基体和掺杂原子的优先保留效应会严重影响掺杂位点的判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂氧化物具有可通过化学掺杂精细调控的电、磁、光学性质，但在低掺杂水平下，对控制性质的单个掺杂原子进行原子尺度分析日益困难。原子探针层析(APT)虽能提供化学灵敏度和空间分辨率以成像低至百万分之几的单个掺杂原子，但要可靠提取这些信息，需要对原子特异性的场蒸发过程有详细的了解。

Method: 采用Zr掺杂的ErMnO3作为模型体系，结合原子探针层析(APT)实验测量与场蒸发模拟，分析掺杂原子与基体原子的原子位置及场蒸发条件。

Result: 研究发现基体和掺杂原子均存在显著的优先保留效应，这会强烈影响掺杂位点的确定，甚至导致错误的解释。该保留效应由内禀参数（如掺杂原子的蒸发场和浓度）和外禀参数（如分析温度）共同决定。

Conclusion: 该结果对基于APT的固体体系中单个掺杂原子分析具有重要意义，并有助于从原子层面理解场蒸发动力学。

Abstract: Complex oxides possess a wide range of electric, magnetic, and optical properties that can be precisely tuned by chemical doping. The atomic-scale analysis of the property-controlling dopants, however, becomes increasingly difficult towards low doping levels. Atom probe tomography (APT) offers chemical sensitivity and spatial resolution to image individual dopant atoms down to a few parts per million. To reliably extract such information, detailed knowledge about the atom-specific field evaporation processes is required. Here we demonstrate a first insight into the APT-measured atomic position of dopant atoms and the field evaporation conditions, using Zr-doped ErMnO3 as a model system. Our analysis reveals a substantial preferential retention of both matrix and dopant atoms which strongly affects the dopant site determination and can lead to an incorrect interpretation. The retention effect is determined by intrinsic and extrinsic parameters, such as the dopant's evaporation field and concentration and the analysis temperature, respectively, as we explain based on field-evaporation simulations. Our results are important for the APT-based analysis of individual dopant atoms in solid systems and the understanding of field evaporation dynamics at the atomic level in general.

</details>


### [115] [Nanometer-scale operando electric field mapping in oxide junctions by correlative STEM-EBIC and 4D-STEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34087)
*Yining Xie,Eoin Moynihan,Xingyao Li,Richard Beanland,Aditya Singh,Marin Alexe,Mingmin Yang,Ana Sanchez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将原位STEM-EBIC与4D-STEM相结合的方法，在纳米尺度定量重构氧化物肖特基结中电场随偏压的空间分布，发现其偏离理想肖特基耗尽模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解氧化物界面局域电场对连接界面静电与器件功能至关重要，但传统电学测量只能间接推断电场，缺乏纳米尺度、工作状态下的直接定量电场映射手段。

Method: 以La0.67Sr0.33MnO3/Nb:SrTiO3（LSMO/NSTO）为模型体系，在MEMS偏压平台上制备电子透明的肖特基结并验证其整流输运行为；结合原位STEM-EBIC（电子束诱导电流）与4D-STEM，互补地定量重建偏压相关的纳米尺度电场空间分布。

Result: 重建的电场分布显示耗尽区内电场呈显著非线性衰减，且电场延伸穿透进入LSMO层，直接揭示了氧化物肖特基结偏离理想经典肖特基耗尽模型，提供了非经典界面静电的实验证据。

Conclusion: 该关联方法实现了工作状态下氧化物异质结的定量纳米尺度电场映射，为将界面静电与宏观输运关联起来以及指导氧化物器件设计提供了基础。

Abstract: Understanding local electric fields at oxide interfaces is essential for linking interfacial electrostatics to device functionality, yet conventional electrical measurements infer these fields only indirectly. Here, we combine operando scanning transmission electron microscope electron-beam-induced current (STEM-EBIC) with four-dimensional-STEM (4D-STEM) to quantitatively reconstruct the bias-dependent electric field spatial distribution across an oxide Schottky junction at nanometer scale. Using La0.67Sr0.33MnO3/Nb:SrTiO3 (LSMO/NSTO) as a model system, we fabricate an electron-transparent junction on a MEMS biasing platform and verify that it retains the rectifying transport behavior. STEM-EBIC provides complementary information addressing several key limitations in quantitative 4D-STEM field mapping for heterojunctions. The reconstructed electric field profiles show a pronounced nonlinear decay within the depletion region and an extended penetration into the LSMO. These results directly reveal the deviations of oxides Schottky junction from the ideal conventional Schottky depletion model, providing experimental signatures of non-classical interfacial electrostatics. Our correlative approach enables quantitative nanoscale electric field mapping in operating oxide heterojunctions, providing a basis for linking interfacial electrostatics to macroscopic transport and guiding oxide devices design.

</details>


### [116] [Finite-temperature grain boundary phases, transformations, and diagrams in alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34131)
*Flynn Walsh,Timofey Frolov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种蒙特卡洛模拟方法，可同时采样界面中的原子位置、构型与原子数，实现了对多组分材料晶界有限温度结构、相变及相图的严格预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合金晶界理解的局限源于原子模拟技术的不完备：它们要么忽略熵的贡献，要么人为约束原子密度，无法完整描述复杂界面的微观自由度。

Method: 引入蒙特卡洛模拟，同时采样复杂界面中所有微观自由度——原子位置、原子构型（组分排布）与原子数目，从而严格刻画有限温度下的晶界行为。

Result: 在W-V合金的模拟中，方法揭示了此前无法触及的一系列相变，包括间隙溶质的凝聚以及位错结构的转变，证明该方法确有必要。

Conclusion: 该方法弥补了传统原子模拟忽略熵或约束密度的缺陷，能为多组分材料提供严格的有限温度晶界结构、相变与相图预测。

Abstract: Current understanding of alloy grain boundaries is limited by the incompleteness of atomistic simulation techniques, which either neglect entropy or artificially constrain the atomic density. This study introduces Monte Carlo simulations that simultaneously sample all the microscopic degrees of freedom in complex interfaces: position, configuration, and atom number. The method enables rigorous predictions of finite-temperature grain boundary structures, phase transformations, and diagrams in multicomponent materials. Simulations of W-V alloys demonstrate the necessity of the approach by uncovering a range of previously inaccessible phase transitions including condensation of interstitial solutes and transformation of dislocation structure.

</details>


### [117] [Magneto-optical Kerr spectroscopy of exciton Rydberg states in a magnetic van der Waals heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34536)
*Astha Khandelwal,Benran Zhang,Madhusmita Jena,Zhenchao Wen,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Saroj P. Dash,Zhenglu Li,Ryo Kitaura,Bhagwati Prasad,Daichi Kozawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用波长分辨磁光克尔光谱研究MoSe₂/Fe₃GaTe₂范德华异质结，发现界面交换作用导致A、B激子系列（基态与2s态）出现磁致对称性破缺，克尔共振极性随磁场反转且A、B激子呈现出相反极性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维半导体中的激子态对时间反演对称性破缺敏感，但界面交换作用如何作用于更高能的激子里德伯态（如2s态及不同激子系列）尚缺乏研究，需要一种能灵敏探测跨激子流形磁致对称性破缺的手段。

Method: 对近邻耦合的MoSe₂/Fe₃GaTe₂范德华异质结进行波长分辨磁光克尔光谱测量，结合反射、光致发光和光致发光激发光谱；并用包含金属Fe₃GaTe₂层屏蔽效应的第一性原理多体计算进行指认；进一步通过磁场奇/偶分解确认共振的磁性起源。

Result: 在A、B激子流形中观测到显著增强的克尔共振，并将其归属为两个系列的基态和2s态；反转磁场使所有共振极性反转，场奇/场偶分解证实其磁性起源；A、B激子流形的克尔极性相反，可归因于近邻感应交换耦合作用于两个激子系列相反的价带自旋。

Conclusion: 共振克尔光谱可作为灵敏探针，用于探测跨激子流形由磁致诱导的对称性破缺。

Abstract: Excitonic states in two-dimensional semiconductors are sensitive to time-reversal symmetry breaking, yet how interfacial exchange acts on higher-lying exciton Rydberg states remains largely unexplored. Here we show that wavelength-resolved magneto-optical Kerr spectroscopy of a proximity-coupled MoSe$_2$/Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ van der Waals heterostructure resolves magnetic symmetry breaking across the A- and B-exciton manifolds. Combining reflection, photoluminescence, and photoluminescence excitation spectroscopy with first-principles many-body calculations including screening by the metallic Fe$_3$GaTe$_2$ layer, we assign the strongly enhanced Kerr resonances to the ground and 2s states of both manifolds. Reversing the magnetic field inverts the polarity of every resonance. A field-odd/field-even decomposition confirms a magnetic origin. The opposite Kerr polarities of the A- and B-exciton manifolds can be explained by proximity-induced exchange coupling to the opposite valence-band spins of the two exciton series. Our results establish resonant Kerr spectroscopy as a sensitive probe of magnetically induced symmetry breaking across the excitonic manifold.

</details>


### [118] [Phase compositions of Si oxynitride: A comprehensive thermodynamic modeling study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34796)
*Andrey Sarikov,Mariia Babiichuk,Mariia Voitovych,Sergii V. Mamykin1*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过热力学分析（吉布斯自由能最低原理）研究了非晶SiOxNy薄膜的相形成规律，构建了随化学成分与制备温度变化的完整相图，揭示了单相与两相区域的边界及临界化学计量比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解SiOxNy薄膜的相组成如何随化学成分和制备温度变化，为实际应用中按需调控薄膜特性提供理论依据。

Method: 基于吉布斯自由能最低原理，对两种可能的相态（均匀单相氮氧化硅、氧化硅与氮化硅两相混合物）进行热力学分析，确定不同化学计量比（0≤x≤2, 0≤y≤4/3）和制备温度下的相组成，并构建综合相图。

Result: 确定了SiOxNy薄膜随化学计量比和制备温度变化的相组成演变规律；揭示了两相氮氧化硅中氧化硅相和氮化硅相的化学组成特征；确定了单相与两相区域间的边界及其临界化学计量比；构建了指示单相与两相区域随制备温度变化的完整相图。

Conclusion: 该研究拓展了对氮氧化硅薄膜基本性质及其对制备条件依赖关系的认识，所得相图与临界条件可用于面向实际应用精确调控SiOxNy薄膜的特性。

Abstract: In this work, a comprehensive thermodynamic description of the formation of phases in amorphous SiOxNy (0 <= x <= 2, 0 <= y <= 4/3) films as a function of the film chemical composition and fabrication temperature is presented. Two possible phase states, namely homogeneous single-phase Si oxynitride and two-phase mixture of Si oxide and Si nitride phases, are analyzed in terms of the lowest values of the Gibbs free energy. The characters of the modification of the phase composition upon changing the SiOxNy stoichiometry indices and fabrication temperature are determined. Moreover, characteristic features of the chemical composition of the Si oxide and Si nitride phases in two-phase Si oxynitrides are revealed. Particular attention is given to the thermodynamic origin of the boundaries between different phase states of the SiOxNy films and to determining the critical stoichiometry values separating them. A comprehensive phase diagram indicating single- and two-phase SiOxNy regions as functions of the fabrication temperature is constructed. The peculiarities of the transitions between different SiOxNy phase states, along with underlying mechanisms, are discussed with reference to this phase diagram. The obtained results extend our understanding of the fundamental properties of Si oxynitride films and their dependence on the film fabrication conditions, and may be used for tailoring SiOxNy characteristics for practical applications.

</details>


### [119] [Chemical site bases and average-atom potentials for the atomic cluster expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34869)
*Lorenzo Piersante,Anirudh Raju Natarajan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究多组分原子团簇展开（ACE）势中化学基（chemical basis）的选择，并建立了拟合线性 ACE 与随机合金平均原子势之间的精确解析映射。通过对 occupational、Chebyshev 和传统 one-hot 三种化学基的基准测试，发现在小数据情形下 occupational 基收敛最快、对目标性质控制最好，且能给出最可靠的平均原子势描述；大数据极限下三种基表现趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACE 是构建原子间势的重要方法，但传统采用 one-hot 编码表示化学自由度，导致模型按化学分层。然而，用于在位晶格构型团簇展开的替代化学表示尚未在原子间势中系统评估。作者旨在厘清化学基的选择如何影响 ACE 势的数据效率与模型性能。

Method: 作者系统回顾了任意化学位点基下的多组分 ACE，并在 occupational、Chebyshev 和传统 one-hot 三种化学基上构造势函数。进一步，通过解析推导建立了拟合线性 ACE 与描述完全随机合金的平均原子势之间的精确映射关系。

Result: 在 Mg-Nd 体系的溶质结合能与空位形成能，以及 Mo-Nb、Cr-W 固溶体混合焓的基准测试中，训练数据稀缺时 occupational 基收敛最快，对目标材料性质的控制最佳，且给出最可靠的随机合金热力学平均原子描述；传统基与 Chebyshev 基在复现这些性质上面临困难。在数据充足时，三种基性能趋于一致。

Conclusion: 化学基是决定数据效率的一项设计选择，其显式处理为浓合金的热力学、力学与动力学性质研究开辟了通向平均原子势的新途径。

Abstract: Interatomic potentials are central tools in the atomistic modeling of materials. The atomic cluster expansion (ACE) parameterizes such potentials from ab initio data, conventionally encoding the chemical degrees of freedom with a one-hot representation that yields chemically stratified models. The alternative chemical representations used in on-lattice configurational cluster expansions have not been assessed for interatomic potentials. Here we revisit the multicomponent ACE for an arbitrary chemical site basis. We then establish an exact analytical mapping between a fitted linear ACE and the average-atom potential that describes a perfectly random alloy. We benchmark potentials built on the occupational, Chebyshev, and conventional ACE bases against solute binding and vacancy formation energies in Mg-Nd, and against the mixing enthalpies of the Mo-Nb and Cr-W solid solutions. When training data are scarce, the occupational basis converges fastest and offers the best control over targeted material properties, while the conventional and Chebyshev bases face challenges in reproducing these properties. The occupational basis likewise yields the most reliable average-atom description of disordered alloy thermodynamics. In the large-data limit the three bases perform identically. The chemical basis is therefore a design choice that governs data efficiency. Its explicit treatment opens a route to average-atom potentials for the thermodynamic, mechanical, and kinetic properties of concentrated alloys.

</details>


### [120] [Optimization of InAs/InAlGaAs/InP quantum dots for O-band laser applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34872)
*Anna Penkała,Paweł Podemski,Vinayakrishna Joshi,Vitalii Sichkovskyi,Johann P. Reithmaier,Grzegorz Sęk*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过分子束外延优化 InP 衬底上的 InAs 量子点发射器，实现了 O 波段激光有源区中基态与激发态之间的大能量分离，从而有效抑制高温下的热载流子逃逸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O 波段兼具低色散和低传输损耗，非常适合光通信；但长波长量子点发射器常因基态与激发态能量分离不足，导致载流子经激发态热逃逸，限制器件性能。

Method: 采用分子束外延在 InP 衬底上生长 InAs 量子点，通过优化设计提升载流子俘获效率并将发射波长调谐至 O 波段，利用吸收光谱表征最终结构以确认基态与激发态的能量分离。

Result: 优化后的结构在量子点基态与激发态之间获得了显著的能量分离，即使在高温下也能有效抑制热载流子逃逸。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了长波长量子点激光器中提升载流子俘获与保持能力的关键设计原则，为高性能 O 波段激光器有源区的开发提供了指导。

Abstract: The O-band telecommunication spectral range combines minimal chromatic dispersion with low transmission loss, making it highly suitable for optical communications. Semiconductor telecom lasers utilizing low-dimensional active regions, such as quantum dots, offer low threshold power and high thermal stability. However, long-wavelength quantum dot emitters often suffer from thermal carrier escape through excited states when the energy separation between the ground and excited states is insufficient. Here we demonstrate the optimization of InAs quantum dot emitters grown on InP by molecular-beam epitaxy for O-band laser active regions. By optimizing the design for better carrier capture efficiency and tuning the emission wavelength to the O-band, we show ways of improving the carrier confinement. Absorption spectroscopy on the finalized structure confirms substantial energy separation between the quantum dot ground and excited states, effectively suppressing thermal carrier escape even at elevated temperatures. These results highlight critical design for improving carrier capture and retention in long-wavelength quantum dot lasers.

</details>


### [121] [First Principles Magnetic Initialisation Made Simple](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35081)
*Kit D. Brown,Robert A. Lawrence*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对称性分析和非磁性能带结构、仅从第一性原理出发且计算成本低廉的方法，用于预测材料的磁结构，无需依赖实验磁初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理电子结构计算的一个根本问题是需要磁初始化，而磁初始化通常依赖于实验结果，这限制了从纯理论角度进行预测的能力。

Method: 该方法基于对称性分析，并仅利用非磁性能带结构（non-magnetic band structure）来生成磁初始化，从而预测材料的磁结构。

Result: 该方法成功再现了多种磁性材料的已知磁基态，包括铁磁性的体心立方 Fe、反铁磁性的 L1₀ PtMn、以及受挫反铁磁性的 L1₂ IrMn₃；同时也正确预测非磁性材料保持非磁性。

Conclusion: 提出一种计算成本低且无需实验初始化的方法，能够仅凭对称性分析和非磁性能带结构可靠预测磁结构，适用于多种磁性材料及非磁性材料。

Abstract: A fundamental issue with first principles electronic structure calculation is the requirement for magnetic initialisations, which are usually derived from experimental results. In this paper, we present a new and computationally inexpensive method to predict magnetic structures from first principles, based upon symmetry analysis and a non-magnetic band structure only. We demonstrate that this method of creating initialisations successfully reproduces the known magnetic ground state for a wide class of magnetic materials, including ferromagnetic BCC Fe, antiferromagnetic L1$_0$ PtMn, and frustrated antiferromagnetic L1$_2$ IrMn$_3$, while also predicting non-magnetic materials to remain non-magnetic.

</details>


### [122] [Machine-learning-guided exploration of domain walls in the hybrid improper ferroelectric Ca3Ti2O7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35346)
*Ida C. Skogvoll,Erik Fransson,Leo Ö. Westin,Benjamin A. D. Williamson,Nicholas C. Bristowe,Sverre M. Selbach,Paul Erhart*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用群论与机器学习原子间势（MLIP）系统研究了Ca3Ti2O7中混合非本征铁电畴壁的复杂结构，并揭示了长程静电作用在建模中的关键意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruddlesden-Popper相因混合非本征铁电机制能产生极化，其畴壁结构涉及八面体旋转、极性畸变与应变等多重序参量的耦合，结构复杂，亟需高精度且可扩展的方法来系统探究。

Method: 以Ca3Ti2O7为原型，结合群论与机器学习原子间势（MLIP），映射{100}、{110}和{001}赝四方平面内的畴壁结构；采用电荷感知训练框架并纳入参考Born有效电荷以修正长程静电，降低畴壁形成能预测误差；并用MLIP在原子尺度寻找最小能量路径。

Result: 训练所得势能对所有考虑的壁类型和取向均复现了DFT的序参量与极化分布；电荷感知框架使畴壁形成能预测误差降低70%，证明长程静电对建模对称破缺界面的重要性；识别出极化翻转的低能反极性构型路径。

Conclusion: 通过在机器学习势中融入长程静电作用，可以高精度、高效率地复现DFT结果，并揭示极化翻转的低能反极性构型，为混合非本征铁电畴壁的理论建模与功能设计提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Ruddlesden-Popper phases are highly tunable and naturally layered structures, in which polarization can arise via a hybrid improper ferroelectric mechanism. This enables a complex domain wall (DW) structure where multiple order parameters, like octahedral rotations, polar distortions and strain, interact. In this work, we explore the rich set of DW structures in prototypical Ca3Ti2O7, mapping out the DWs in the {100}, {110} and {001} pseudo-tetragonal planes using group theory and machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs). The trained potential reproduces the density functional theory (DFT) order parameter and polarization profiles for all wall types and orientations considered. A charge-aware training framework combined with reference Born effective charges reduces the prediction errors for the DW formation energies by 70%, revealing the importance of including long-range electrostatics to model symmetry-broken interfaces. Finally, the MLIP is used to identify minimum energy pathways at the atomic scale with nearly the precision of DFT calculations, revealing a low-energy antipolar configuration for polarization switching.

</details>


### [123] [High-Throughput Imaging of Degradation-Inducing Microscopic Impurities in Perovskite Solar Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35510)
*Sofiia Kosar,Anil R. Pininti,Vladyslav Hnapovskyi,José P. Jurado,Subhashri Mannar,Lorenzo Mardegan,Anand S. Subbiah,Frédéric Laquai,Stefaan De Wolf*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一套基于高分辨率反射光显微与机器学习图像分析的快速诊断框架，可在数秒内检测并量化宽带隙钙钛矿薄膜中的有害残余杂质，并将杂质密度作为稳定性预警指标，用于钙钛矿太阳能电池的规模化预筛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量大面积钙钛矿薄膜的可规模化制造受到微观不均匀性（尤其是加工过程中形成的残余成分杂质）的阻碍。要推进钙钛矿太阳能电池的产业化放大，必须识别这些杂质、理解其对器件运行的影响，并实现快速检测。

Method: 采用纳米傅里叶变换红外光谱（nano-FTIR）结合高分辨率光学与扫描探针技术，识别宽带隙钙钛矿薄膜中的有害杂质；通过光应力实验揭示杂质-钙钛矿界面处的退化起始机制；进而构建基于高分辨率反射光显微镜和机器学习图像分析的快速、非侵入、高通量检测框架，并将该框架扩展用于退化追踪。

Result: 光应力实验表明钙钛矿层的退化始于杂质-钙钛矿界面，杂质充当失效成核位点；所开发的框架可在数秒内检测并量化刚制备薄膜中的有害杂质；杂质密度更高的薄膜表现出加速的早期退化，且退化追踪揭示了光化学与热化学耦合对杂质介导不稳定性的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一套经过化学验证的实用诊断成像框架，用于钙钛矿薄膜的快速预筛，为稳定、可规模化的钙钛矿太阳能电池提供了可行的质量评估手段。

Abstract: The scalable fabrication of high-quality, large-area perovskite thin films is hindered by microscopic inhomogeneities, particularly residual compositional impurities formed during processing. Identifying these impurities, understanding their impact on device operation, and enabling their rapid detection are essential for upscaling perovskite solar cells (PSCs). Here, nano-Fourier transform infrared spectroscopy combined with high-resolution optical and scanning probe techniques was used to identify detrimental impurities in wide-bandgap perovskite films relevant to tandem solar cells. Photo-stress experiments revealed that degradation of the perovskite layer initiates at impurity-perovskite interfaces, demonstrating that impurities act as failure nucleation sites. Leveraging these insights, a rapid, non-invasive, and high-throughput framework based on high-resolution reflected light microscopy and machine learning-supported image analysis was developed, enabling detection and quantification of harmful impurities in as-prepared films within seconds. Films with higher impurity density show accelerated early degradation, establishing this parameter as an early-warning metric for stability screening. Extending this framework to degradation tracking further reveals coupled photo- and thermo-chemical contributions to impurity-mediated instability. Overall, this work establishes a practical chemically-validated diagnostic imaging framework for rapid pre-screening of perovskite films to enable stable and scalable PSCs.

</details>


### [124] [Ultra-high vacuum Raman platform for in situ characterization of graphene nanoribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35556)
*Jeong Ha Hwang,Amogh Kinikar,Lukas Rotach,Andres Ortega-Guerrero,Carlo A. Pignedoli,Klaus Muellen,Thorsten G. Englmann,Xinliang Feng,Pascal Ruffieux,Roman Fasel,Mickael L. Perrin,Gabriela Borin Barin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种便携式超高真空拉曼真空箱（RVS），用于在免暴露于空气的条件下原位研究空气敏感的原子级精确石墨烯纳米带（GNR），实现对7/9-AGNR表面合成、宽温区晶格动力学及受控O2暴露下反应性边缘化学的原位监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯纳米带中未配对的π电子赋予其磁性，但也使其在空气中极易化学降解。一旦暴露于空气，常规表征只能探测氧化或化学修饰后的物种，而非本征形态。因此核心挑战是：在不破坏真空的前提下，获取空气敏感GNR在合成过程中及可控环境下的振动与电子本征信号。

Method: 作者构建了一套自制的移动式超高真空拉曼装置——拉曼真空箱（RVS），集成了温度控制与精密气体配给功能，可实现在明确的热学与化学环境条件下，对生长动力学、晶格动力学及反应性边缘响应进行原位监测。利用该平台对7原子宽和9原子宽扶手椅型GNR（7-AGNR、9-AGNR）的表面合成、7-AGNR在162-748 K宽温区的拉曼模式演化，以及受控O2暴露下的化学变化进行了研究。

Result: 成功原位监测了7-与9-AGNR的表面合成过程；量化了7-AGNR拉曼模式在162-748 K范围内的温度演化；分辨出受控O2暴露下的化学变化，且该变化与锯齿形（zigzag）反应位点的氧化一致。

Conclusion: UHV拉曼光谱与RVS的结合，为在可控环境中获取低维量子材料本征振动信号提供了一条可行路径，克服了空气敏感材料在表征前发生降解的难题。

Abstract: Atomically precise graphene nanoribbons (GNRs) exhibit tunable electronic and magnetic properties governed by edge topology and finite-size effects, which make them versatile platforms for next-generation electronic and spintronic applications. However, the unpaired pi-electrons responsible for their magnetic properties simultaneously make them highly susceptible to chemical degradation under ambient conditions. This intrinsic reactivity poses a central experimental challenge: accessing vibrational and electronic signatures of air-sensitive GNRs during synthesis and under controlled environments without breaking vacuum. Once the material has been exposed to air, standard characterization techniques would probe oxidized or chemically modified species rather than the pristine form. Here, we overcome this limitation by developing a home-built ultra-high vacuum (UHV) Raman platform designed to preserve sample integrity by preventing air exposure and to enable in situ investigation of material properties. The portable Raman vacuum suitcase (RVS) integrates temperature control and precise gas dosing, allowing direct monitoring of growth kinetics, lattice dynamics, and reactive-edge responses under well-defined thermal and chemical environments. Using this platform, we monitor the on-surface synthesis of 7- and 9-atom-wide armchair GNRs (7- and 9-AGNRs), quantify the evolution of 7-AGNR Raman modes over a wide temperature range (162-748 K), and resolve chemical changes upon controlled O2 exposure that are consistent with oxidation at the reactive zigzag sites. These results establish UHV Raman spectroscopy with the RVS as a route to accessing the intrinsic vibrational signatures of low-dimensional quantum materials under controlled environments.

</details>


### [125] [AI-Assisted Identification of Magnetic Orders and Skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35566)
*Haowen Yang,Sophia Huerta,Yingying Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个机器学习框架，利用 Materials Project 的结构、成分和电子信息，对二维材料进行铁磁/反铁磁分类与磁化强度预测，并结合模拟磁成像与拓扑电荷分析筛选具有斯格明子等拓扑磁态的高温候选材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料中的奇异磁有序对能效型自旋电子学应用极具吸引力，但实现稳健的高温范德华反铁磁体与拓扑磁态仍具挑战，亟需高效筛选方法。

Method: 构建两类定长描述符：用 LightGBM 进行 FM/AFM 分类（仅基于结构衍生描述符,不显式包含磁性描述符），采用按化学体系分组的五折交叉验证以防化学泄漏并优化超参数和分类阈值；用残差多层感知机（MLP）结合成分、结构、电子及任务特异的初始态描述符预测磁化强度，通过对数目标变换和鲁棒加权损失处理宽分布，并集成三个独立训练模型。

Result: 孤立测试集上分类器平衡准确率达 93.6%；磁化强度预测模型平均绝对误差为 0.686 μB/化学式单位。对选定候选材料的模拟磁成像和相位重构分析揭示了磁化织构及类斯格明子特征（通过归一化磁化轮廓与拓扑电荷表征）。

Conclusion: 该框架为筛选二维磁性材料、优先确定反铁磁与拓扑自旋电子学候选材料提供了一种高效途径。

Abstract: Exotic magnetic orders in two-dimensional (2D) materials are attracting huge interest for energy-efficient spintronic applications, yet realizing robust high-temperature van der Waals antiferromagnets and topological magnetic states remains challenging. In this work, we develop a machine-learning framework for identifying magnetic orders and predicting magnetization using structural, compositional, and electronic information derived from the Materials Project. Fixed-length descriptors are constructed for two complementary tasks: ferromagnetic (FM) versus antiferromagnetic (AFM) classification and quantitative magnetization prediction. Magnetic order is classified using a LightGBM model trained on structure-derived descriptors without explicitly including magnetic descriptors. Five-fold cross-validation grouped by chemical system is used to reduce chemical leakage, together with hyperparameter and classification-threshold optimization. On an isolated test set, the classifier achieves a balanced accuracy of 93.6% in the testing set. Magnetization is predicted using a residual multilayer perceptron with compositional, structural, electronic, and task-specific initial-state descriptors. A logarithmic target transformation and robust weighted loss are used to account for the broad magnetization distribution, while three independently trained models are combined into a final ensemble. The model achieves a mean absolute error of 0.686 μB/formula unit. For selected magnetic candidates, simulated magnetic imaging and phase-reconstruction analysis are further used to investigate magnetization textures and skyrmion-like features through normalized magnetization profiles and topological charge. This framework provides an efficient approach for screening 2D magnetic materials and prioritizing candidates for antiferromagnetic and topological spintronic applications.

</details>


### [126] [Density functional perturbation theory of meta-generalized gradient approximations using algorithmic differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35572)
*Bruno Ploumhans,Niklas Frederik Schmitz,Michael F. Herbst*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出将DFPT扩展至依赖于密度ρ和动能密度τ的meta-GGA（及更广泛非局域）交换关联泛函，并通过算法微分（AD）将XC二阶导数重新表述为Jacobi-向量积，从而避免繁琐的解析推导；结合既有的AD-DFPT框架，实现对ZnO和BaTiO3的多种响应性质计算，并用该梯度训练神经网络meta-GGA以自洽复现混合DFT参考密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFPT是平面波密度泛函理论中计算导数的成熟框架，但现有实现主要针对仅依赖密度的LDA/GGA泛函。对于显式依赖动能密度τ的meta-GGA等半局域泛函，其二阶XC能量导数的解析推导极为繁琐，阻碍了meta-GGA级响应性质的计算以及基于梯度的新型泛函训练。

Method: 将XC二阶能量导数重新表述为XC势的Jacobi-向量积，并利用算法微分（AD）技术求值，从而绕过繁琐的解析二阶导数。该方法集成到作者此前开发的AD-DFPT框架中，可对任意基态量关于任意微扰求导。实验上对ZnO和BaTiO3计算多种响应性质，并以体硅为例，用AD-DFPT梯度优化一个神经网络meta-GGA，使其自洽复现混合DFT参考密度。

Result: 在ZnO和BaTiO3的响应性质计算中，最近的r2SCAN01 meta-GGA泛函总体上优于LDA和PBE；神经网络meta-GGA经AD-DFPT梯度优化后能够自洽复现体硅的混合DFT参考密度。这些结果表明AD-DFPT是计算meta-GGA级DFT导数的通用途径，既适用于常规响应性质，也适用于新型XC泛函梯度训练所需的非常规导数。

Conclusion: AD-DFPT为meta-GGA级别的DFT导数计算提供了通用且灵活的路径，无论是常见响应性质还是泛函训练所需的特殊导数，均能在统一框架下高效实现。

Abstract: Density functional perturbation theory (DFPT) is an established framework for the computation of derivatives in plane-wave density functional theory. We present an implementation of DFPT for exchange-correlation (XC) functionals $E_\mathrm{xc}(ρ,τ)$ that incorporate an explicit dependence on both the density $ρ$ and the kinetic energy density $τ$. This covers the popular class of semilocal meta-generalized gradient approximations (meta-GGAs) as well as broader nonlocal parametrizations. We sidestep the derivation of cumbersome XC second energy derivative expressions by recasting these derivatives as a Jacobian-vector product of the XC potentials, which we evaluate with algorithmic differentiation (AD) techniques. Integration with our previously developed AD-DFPT framework [N. F. Schmitz et al., npj Comput. Mater. 12, 6 (2026)] provides access to derivatives of arbitrary ground state quantities with respect to arbitrary perturbations. We employ AD-DFPT to compute a range of response properties for ZnO and BaTiO3, and find that the recent r2SCAN01 meta-GGA functional generally outperforms LDA and PBE. Finally, we showcase the optimization of a neural-network meta-GGA to self-consistently reproduce hybrid-DFT reference densities of bulk silicon, using AD-DFPT gradients. Overall, these results establish AD-DFPT as a versatile route for computing DFT derivatives at the meta-GGA level, be they common response properties or the unusual derivatives required for the gradient-based training of novel XC functionals.

</details>


### [127] [Pyroelectric thermo-wave probing of BaZr0.2Ti0.8O3 ceramics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35667)
*Nicholas V. Morozovsky,Yuriy O. Zagorodniy,Mykola Yelisieiev,Oleksandr S. Pylypchuk,Mykola O. Semenenko,Iryna V. Kondakova,Lesya P. Yurchenko,Viktor Bovtun,Valentin V. Laguta,Eugene A. Eliseev,Volodymyr O. Dzhagan,Anna N. Morozovska*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文首次采用热波探测技术研究了BaZr0.2Ti0.8O3陶瓷的热释电性质，发现其热释电响应具有强烈的不对称性，并随电场循环历史显著变化，表明存在非遍历弛豫型极性态；结合XRD、拉曼和介电温谱分析，证明该材料在铁电-顺电相变处同时共存有序铁电态与弛豫态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BaZr1-xTiO3（0.15<x<0.25）陶瓷因高介电常数、低漏电流和低损耗因子而适用于可调电容器、多层陶瓷电容器和压电致动器，且因Ti4+被较大离子半径的Zr4+等价位取代产生的局部化学应变而呈现弛豫特性。然而x≈0.2附近的BaZr1-xTiO3陶瓷的热释电性质此前未被研究，本文旨在填补这一知识空白。

Method: 采用固相合成法制备BaZr0.2Ti0.8O3陶瓷，并对其进行热释电热波探测，研究极化态、退极化态、弛豫态和恢复极化态等极性状态；辅以室温X射线衍射谱分析宏观四方性与(200)峰不对称性、拉曼光谱观测714 cm⁻¹谱带随温度升高强度的相对降低，以及介电常数随温度变化并拟合修正的居里-外斯定律。

Result: 热波探测显示陶瓷具有显著的热释电响应，且相对陶瓷两相对表面呈强不对称性；响应轮廓与不对称性强烈依赖于电场循环的预历史，暗示样品中存在非遍历弛豫型极性态。室温XRD显示几乎不存在宏观四方性、(200)峰仅微弱不对称，说明四方性仅局限于富Ti的极性纳米微区内。拉曼714 cm⁻¹谱带随升温相对强度下降，证明在30–40℃之间存在弥散的铁电-顺电相变。介电温谱遵循幂指数为1.4的修正居里-外斯定律，表明材料中有序铁电态与弛豫态共存。

Conclusion: BaZr0.2Ti0.8O3陶瓷呈现出显著且不对称的热释电响应，其性质受电场循环历史影响，对应非遍历弛豫型极性态；材料的宏观结构近乎立方，极性四方性存在于富Ti纳米微区内，且在30–40℃发生弥散相变，表现出有序铁电与弛豫共存的特征。

Abstract: BaxZr1-xTiO3 ceramics with 0.15 < x < 0.25, which have high dielectric permittivity, small leakage currents and low dissipation factor, are promising materials for tunable capacitor devices, multilayered ceramic capacitors and piezoelectric actuators. These materials reveal relaxor properties due to the local chemical strains caused by the isovalent substitution of Ti4+ ions by Zr4+ ions with a larger ionic size. However, to the best of our knowledge, the pyroelectric properties of BaxZr1-xTiO3 ceramics with x~0.2 have not been studied. To fill the gap in knowledge, we perform the pyroelectric thermo-wave probing of BaZr0.2Ti0.8O3 ceramics prepared by the solid-state synthesis. Results of the thermo-wave probing of the most interesting polar states, namely polarized, depolarized, relaxed and restored polarized states, reveal the pronounced pyroelectric response that can be strongly asymmetric with respect to the opposite surfaces of the ceramics. The asymmetry and profiles of pyroelectric response depend significantly on the pre-history of the electric field cycling indicating possible non-ergodic relaxor-type polar states in the ceramic sample. X-ray diffraction spectrum, recorded at room temperature, reveals the virtual absence of the macroscopic tetragonality and the weak asymmetry of the (200) peak, which indicate the small tetragonality inside the Ti-enriched polar nanoregions. Decrease of the relative intensity of the Raman band at 714 cm-1 occurring upon heating evidences the diffuse ferroelectric-paraelectric phase transition between 30 C and 40 C. The temperature dependence of the dielectric permittivity obeys modified Curie-Weiss law with power 1.4, indicating that both ordered ferroelectric and relaxor states coexist in the BaTi0.8Zr0.2O3 ceramics.

</details>


### [128] [A Si-on-SiC Platform for Interfacing with Vanadium Dopants in the Telecom O-Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35702)
*Timothy Draher,Nolan Bitner,Vasileios Niaouris,Claire E. McDermott,David Czaplewski,Supratik Guha,David D. Awschalom,F. Joseph Heremans,Alan M. Dibos*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出硅-碳化硅异质光子平台，通过硅纳米腔与钒色心倏逝耦合，实现通信O波段高效光子提取与量子网络节点构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳化硅中的钒色心具备通信O波段发射、亚微秒光学寿命和秒级自旋弛豫时间，是量子网络节点的理想候选；但SiC高折射率导致光子提取效率低，制约实际应用。

Method: 作者构建硅上碳化硅（Si-on-SiC）异质光子平台，让硅纳米腔与商用4H-SiC中的浅层钒掺杂发生倏逝耦合，在共振激发下实现零声子线的高效收集；并通过单透镜光纤集成905 nm重泵浦激光恢复钒电荷态。

Result: 在原生掺杂样品中观察到Purcell增强光致发光，微秒尺度上瞬态光谱烧孔线宽为50 MHz；在稀注入样品中隔离出单个钒中心，获得高纯度单光子发射和109 ns的Purcell增强寿命；905 nm重泵浦激光以95%效率恢复钒电荷态。

Conclusion: Si-on-SiC平台具有可扩展性和代工兼容性，为通信波长自旋-光子界面提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vanadium color centers in silicon carbide (SiC) offer telecom O-band emission, sub-microsecond optical lifetimes, and second-scale spin relaxation times, rendering them promising candidates for quantum network nodes. However, efficient photon extraction from the high-refractive-index SiC host remains challenging. Here, we introduce a silicon-on-SiC heterogeneous photonic platform in which silicon nanocavities evanescently couple to shallow vanadium dopants in commercial 4H-SiC, enabling efficient zero-phonon line (ZPL) collection under resonant excitation. In as-grown ensembles, we observe Purcell-enhanced photoluminescence with transient spectral hole burning linewidths of 50 MHz on microsecond timescales. In dilute implanted samples, we isolate individual vanadium centers with high-purity single photon emission and a Purcell-enhanced lifetime of 109 ns. The entire optical interface operates through a single lensed fiber, including a 905 nm repump laser that recovers the vanadium charge state with 95% efficiency. These results establish Si-on-SiC as a scalable, foundry-compatible platform for telecom-wavelength spin-photon interfaces.

</details>


### [129] [Catalogue of Collinear Magnetic Structures in Stoichiometric Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35712)
*Liangliang Huang,Houhao Wang,Yuanze Song,Ruixi Pu,Xiangang Wan,Feng Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过自旋空间群（SSG）对共线磁性结构进行穷举枚举，并结合第一性原理计算，构建了大规模共线磁体数据库并系统开展磁拓扑分类，发现数千种新的磁拓扑材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性材料磁结构（MS）的可靠确定通常依赖中子散射等实验，费时费力；而实验数据库 MAGNDATA 中约一半磁结构是共线的，且超80%的共线磁体对应于非磁群的极大共线自旋空间群子群。作者希望以群论预设候选共线磁结构，并大规模计算确定基态自旋空间群对称性，从而加速磁结构表征并推动磁拓扑材料发现。

Method: 采用自旋空间群（SSG）作为对称性框架，对230个非磁空间群的极大共线SSG子群进行穷举，枚举所有在Wyckoff位置形成的共线磁结构；对无机晶体结构数据库（ICSD）中72,075个非磁化学计量比结构进行全对称性允许的共线磁结构分类，得到365,639个共线磁结构；随后对21,315个选定材料中的7,824个共线磁体进行第一性原理计算确定其基态SSG对称性，最后结合SSG和磁空间群（MSG）进行拓扑分类。

Result: 对ICSD中72,075个非磁化学计量比结构穷举出365,639个对称性允许的共线磁结构；通过第一性原理计算确定了21,315个选定材料中7,824个共线磁体的基态SSG对称性；对这些磁体进行拓扑分类后输出数千种新的磁性拓扑材料。MAGNDATA中实验确定的磁结构仅是计算数据库的“冰山一角”。

Conclusion: 通过SSG群论穷举与第一性原理计算相结合，成功构建大规模共线磁体计算数据库并识别出数千种新磁拓扑材料，该数据库可指导实验学家的磁结构表征并推动自旋电子学应用的进一步研究。

Abstract: Magnetic materials persistently serve as a fertile platform for exploring novel quantum emergent phenom ena and advancing information technologies. Reliably determining their magnetic structures (MSs), which is essential for understanding their emergent quantum properties, usually depends on experiments such as neutron scattering. Here, noting that around half of the MSs in MAGNDATA (a database of experimentally determined MSs) are collinear, we adopt the spin space groups (SSGs) to preset the candidate MSs for a given nonmagnetic crystal structure to be collinear from the outset. On the other hand, more than 80% of the collinear magnets in MAGNDATA are subject to maximal subgroups of nonmagnetic groups which are collinear SSGs, and we then enumerate all collinear MSs formed at Wyckoff positions by the maximal collinear SSG subgroups of the 230 nonmagnetic space groups. With such exhaustive enumeration, we categorize all symmetry-allowed collinear MSs for the 72,075 nonmagnetic stoichiometric structures in Inorganic Crystal Structure Database, obtaining 365,639 collinear MSs. We then determine the ground-state SSG symmetries of 7,824 collinear magnets among the 21,315 selected materials using first-principles calculations. These magnets then undergo a comprehensive topological classification combining SSGs and magnetic space groups, outputting thousands of new magnetic topological materials. The experimentally determined MSs in MAGNDATA represent the tip of the iceberg in our computational database of collinear magnets, expected to guide MS characterization by experimentalists and further investigation towards spintronics applications.

</details>
