### [130] [Locality of KMS states for infinite fermionic lattice systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31858)
*Lennart Becker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无限费米子格点系统热平衡态对哈密顿量微扰的响应，核心结论是：在指数衰减关联与指数局部不可区分性条件下，广延微扰后的态呈现拉伸指数衰减的关联，且不要求初态或末态唯一，从而可在相共存情形下研究稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无限费米子格点系统热平衡态（KMS态）在哈密顿量微扰下的稳定性行为，特别关注相共存（即态不唯一）情形下的稳定性问题。现有方法通常依赖态的某种唯一性或特定衰减条件，作者希望放宽这些限制，建立更一般的框架。

Method: 直接在底层C*-代数上建立量子置信传播（QBP）方法，并结合Lieb-Robinson界。通过QBP与Lieb-Robinson界的结合，建立关联衰减、局部微扰局部扰动原理与局部不可区分性之间的蕴含关系，从而在给定的衰减、支撑与一致性假设下闭合迹态微扰的循环。对满足一致聚类条件的广延微扰，还构造了局域平衡期望的可微路径。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 对选定KMS态的广延微扰，指数衰减关联与指数局部不可区分性可推出微扰态具有拉伸指数衰减的关联；(2) 不要求初始态或微扰态唯一，可在相共存下研究稳定性；(3) 对迹态微扰，在给定条件下闭合了关联衰减、局部微扰局部扰动与局部不可区分性之间的循环；(4) 广延微扰在一致聚类条件下得到局域平衡期望的可微路径，其变化量可按微扰的相互作用范数线性有界。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个不依赖态唯一性的框架，将量子置信传播直接建立在C*-代数上并结合Lieb-Robinson界，成功刻画了广延微扰下热平衡态的稳定性，并阐明了关联衰减、局部微扰局部扰动与局部不可区分性之间的内在逻辑关系。

Abstract: We study how thermal equilibrium states of infinite fermionic lattices respond to perturbations of the Hamiltonian. Our main result concerns extensive perturbations of a chosen KMS state. We show that exponential decay of correlations and exponential local indistinguishability under the extensive perturbation yield stretched-exponential decay of correlations for the perturbed state. Neither the initial nor the perturbed state is required to be unique, allowing stability to be studied in the presence of phase coexistence. Our approach uses Quantum Belief Propagation (QBP), which we establish directly on the underlying $C^*$-algebra. Combining QBP with Lieb--Robinson bounds, we show implications between decay of correlations, the principle that local perturbations perturb locally, and local indistinguishability, closing the cycle for perturbations of the tracial state under the stated decay, support and uniformity assumptions. For extensive perturbations under uniform clustering conditions, we also obtain a differentiable path of local equilibrium expectations and bound their change linearly in the interaction norm of the perturbation.

</details>


### [131] [Local Determinant Defects and Low-Energy Subspace Stability in Semifinite von Neumann Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32095)
*Seyed Mahmoud Manjegani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种有限温度下低能谱子空间稳定性的判别方法，核心是局部行列式缺陷与谱展平原理，可对两个 Gibbs 态的低能投影之间的迹 L2 距离给出上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断低能谱子空间（如基态子空间）在不同哈密顿量或有限温度下的稳定性，且希望不要求哈密顿量在算子范数意义下接近，并能处理无界哈密顿量与无穷能级情形。

Method: 作者使用 alpha-z Renyi 核的局部行列式缺陷作为判据，并建立半有限冯·诺依曼代数中有界正定单射算子的“谱展平原理”，把局部乘积缺陷归约为精确的两投影公式。对紧致（含迹类 Gibbs 算子）的下有界隶属哈密顿量，给出迹 L2 距离上界；在矩阵情形得到主角余项之和，量子比特情形则用缺陷与热隙精确确定基态重叠，并对有效低能模型中的有界可观测量给出均匀误差界。

Result: 证明了有限温度判据对下半有界、具有迹类 Gibbs 算子的隶属哈密顿量成立，无需哈密顿量在算子范数下接近；给出无穷能级、无界哈密顿量的例子；对矩阵得到主角余项和，对量子比特得到精确的基态重叠表示与均匀误差界；同时证明行列式不等式、其等式成立条件以及 z-单调性在矩阵、有限与半有限情形成立。

Conclusion: 该有限温度判据借助局部行列式缺陷与谱展平原理，能有效估计低能投影间的迹 L2 距离，广泛适用于无界哈密顿量及无穷维情形，并为基态重叠、有效低能模型误差与行列式不等式性质提供了系统而精确的刻画。

Abstract: We give a finite-temperature test for the stability of low-energy spectral subspaces. The test uses a local determinant defect for the alpha-z Renyi kernel of two Gibbs states and bounds the tracial L2 distance between their low-energy projections. The main tool is a spectral-flattening principle for bounded positive injective operators in a semifinite von Neumann algebra. It reduces the local product defect to an exact two-projection formula. The thermal result applies to lower-bounded affiliated Hamiltonians with trace-class Gibbs operators and does not require the Hamiltonians to be close in operator norm. We give an infinite-level example with unbounded Hamiltonians. For matrices, the result gives a sum of principal-angle remainders. For qubits, the defect and the thermal gaps determine the ground-state overlap exactly. The same estimate gives a uniform error bound for bounded observables in an effective low-energy model. We also prove the determinant inequalities, their equality cases, and z-monotonicity in matrix, finite, and semifinite settings.

</details>


### [132] [The Hamilton--Jacobi and Symplectic Analysis for Extended Hořava Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32218)
*Diana Vanessa Castro-Luna,Alberto Escalante*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文对线性化的非投影 $λR$ 模型（含 BPS 最低阶项及 Cotton 张量平方高阶项）进行了 Hamilton-Jacobi 分析与 Faddeev-Jackiw 量子化，分别讨论 λ≠1/3 与 λ=1/3 两种情形，得到两套框架下一致的基本括号、规范变换及量子路径积分测度，并比较了两种情形在路径积分层面的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对线性化、非投影的 λR 引力模型（并加入 BPS 最低阶项与 Cotton 张量平方高阶项）进行约束分析和量子化，旨在系统建立该理论的哈密顿结构、规范对称性与量子路径积分测度，并澄清 λ≠1/3 与 λ=1/3 两个特殊参数区域在动力学和量子化上的差别。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：(1) Hamilton-Jacobi 分析，求出完整哈密顿量集合、广义括号、基本微分方程、特征方程及规范变换；(2) Faddeev-Jackiw 方法，构造辛张量并由此识别基本括号，进而计算量子路径积分的测度。并对 λ≠1/3 与 λ=1/3 分别处理。

Result: HJ 与 FJ 两种框架得到的基本括号完全一致；由辛张量求出了量子路径积分测度。λ≠1/3 与 λ=1/3 两种情形在量子路径积分上存在差异，体现了参数取值对理论约束结构与量子化结果的影响。

Conclusion: HJ 与 FJ 分析给出了自洽且一致的约束结构与括号，成功完成该扩展非投影 λR 模型的量子化，并揭示 λ=1/3（与 λ≠1/3）在量子路径积分层面的关键区别。

Abstract: The Hamilton-Jacobi [HJ] analysis and the Faddeev-Jackiw [FJ] quantization for the linearized, non-projectable $λR$ model, extended by the lowest-order term of Blas, Pujolàs, and Sibiryakov and the higher-order term involving the square of the Cotton tensor, were carried out. The cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ are analyzed. Within the Hamilton-Jacobi framework, we obtain the complete set of Hamiltonians, generalized brackets, fundamental differential, characteristic equations, and gauge transformations of the theory. In the Faddeev-Jackiw framework, a symplectic tensor is constructed from which the fundamental brackets are identified. The HJ and FJ brackets coincide, and from the symplectic tensor, the measure for the quantum path integral is calculated. The differences between the cases $λ\neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $λ= \frac{1}{3}$ at the quantum path integral are discussed.

</details>


### [133] [Absolute and relative invariants for low-dimensional topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32442)
*Federico Manzoni,Domenico Monaco,Gabriele Peluso*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在拓扑绝缘体背景下，系统分析了定义在 d 维环面（d ≤ 2）上、满足 Altland-Zirnbauer-Cartan（AZC）对称性的投影值映射的分类方案，比较了 Murray-von Neumann、酉等价与同伦等价三种等价关系，揭示了它们各自对应的绝对与相对拓扑不变量（Chern 数与 Fu-Kane-Mele 不变量等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受拓扑绝缘体研究的启发，作者希望厘清在 AZC 对称性约束下，对低维环面上的投影值映射来说，Murray-von Neumann 等价、酉等价与同伦等价这三种等价概念之间的异同，从而为低维能隙量子系统建立一个统一的几何分类框架。

Method: 在“伪周期性”约束下为投影的值域构造对称基，并允许部分基向量绕环面一周时获得一个相位因子。对 d=1 情形证明所有 AZC 类都存在全周期对称基；对 d=2 情形分析拓扑障碍，用 Z（Chern 数）与 Z_2（Fu-Kane-Mele）不变量刻画非平凡相位，并区分环境希尔伯特空间为最小维数与非最小维数时的情形来逐一比较三种等价关系。

Result: d=1 时，所有 AZC 类均存在全周期对称基。d=2 时，出现以 Z（Chern 数）或 Z_2（Fu-Kane-Mele）不变量刻画的非平凡相位，这些构成同时分类 Murray-von Neumann 等价与酉等价的绝对不变量。同伦等价的行为依赖环境希尔伯特空间：非最小维数空间上，同伦等价直接归结为酉等价；在最小环境空间中则出现额外的相对同伦不变量，其具体取值在酉共轭下会改变，因此只有映射之间的相对拓扑相位是有良好定义的。

Conclusion: 该工作为低维能隙量子系统提供了一个统一的几何框架，说明不同的等价关系如何生成特定的绝对与相对拓扑不变量，深化了对拓扑绝缘体分类的理解。

Abstract: [An extended abstract is provided in-text]
  Motivated by topological insulators, this paper analyzes classification schemes for projection-valued maps on $d$-dimensional tori, $d \le 2$, subject to Altland-Zirnbauer-Cartan (AZC) symmetries (time-reversal, particle-hole, and chiral). The main objective is to compare three notions of equivalence for these maps: Murray-von Neumann, unitary, and homotopy equivalence. The analysis relies on constructing symmetric bases for the projection ranges under a pseudo-periodicity constraint, where select basis vectors pick up a phase when looped around the torus. If $d=1$, fully periodic symmetric bases exist across all AZC classes. If $d=2$, topological obstructions appear as non-trivial phases characterized by $\mathbb{Z}$ (Chern number) or $\mathbb{Z}_2$ (Fu-Kane-Mele) invariants. These serve as absolute invariants that classify both Murray-von Neumann and unitary equivalences. For homotopy equivalence, the behavior depends on the ambient Hilbert space: if this has non-minimal dimension, homotopy equivalence reduces directly to unitary equivalence, while in minimal ambient spaces additional relative homotopy invariants emerge. Their precise values change under unitary conjugation, leaving only relative topological phases between maps well-defined. Ultimately, this provides a unified geometric framework for gapped quantum systems in low dimensions, demonstrating how distinct equivalence relations generate specific absolute and relative topological invariants.

</details>


### [134] [Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization for fractional and long-range Anderson models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33084)
*Peter D. Hislop,Rodrigo Matos,Constanza Rojas-Molina*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究长程Anderson模型（含分数Anderson模型）在强无序下的局域本征值统计与动力学（去）局域化性质，证明当β>d/2时本征值统计服从泊松点过程，且不存在动力学局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统（最近邻跳跃的）Anderson模型在强无序下呈现泊松本征值统计与动力学局域化，但对具有长程跳跃（如分数Laplacian）的Anderson模型，其局域本征值统计与动力学局域化之间的关系尚不清楚。Disertori等人曾猜想强无序下这些长程模型不存在动力学局域化，本文旨在验证这一猜想。

Method: 对长程跳跃项满足|T(n,m)|~||n-m||^{-(d+2β)}（β>0）的Anderson型随机扰动，在强随机位势下建立Green函数分数矩的显式、锐利下界，从而证明位置算子的高阶矩无穷大；并据此证明本征值统计为以态密度n(E)为强度的泊松点过程。

Result: 在强无序且β>d/2时，以确定性谱中任意能量E为中心的局域本征值统计为强度n(E)的泊松点过程（几乎必然）；同时证明强无序下不存在动力学局域化，即动力学退局域化。特别地，在d=1维，分数Anderson模型（(-Δ)^α的随机扰动，α∈(1/2,1)）呈现泊松本征值统计与动力学退局域化。

Conclusion: 长程Anderson模型在强无序下呈现泊松本征值统计与动力学退局域化并存的现象，与仅最近邻跳跃的标准Anderson模型形成鲜明对比（后者强无序下为泊松统计且动力学局域化），验证了Disertori等人的猜想。

Abstract: We study local eigenvalue statistics and dynamical (de)-localization properties for long-range Anderson models, including the fractional Anderson model. These systems are random Anderson-type perturbations of operators with long-range (non random) hopping terms of the form $|T(n,m)| \sim \|n-m\|^{-(d+2β)}$ for $β>0$, on the $d$-dimensional lattice. In the presence of a strong enough random potential, these models exhibit dense pure point spectrum with polynomially decaying eigenvectors, almost surely. We show that in this strong disorder regime, and for $β>d/2$, the local eigenvalue statistics centered at any $E$ in the deterministic spectrum is a Poisson point process with intensity given by the density of states function $n(E)$. Moreover, we prove that, at strong disorder, there is no dynamical localization for these models, verifying a conjecture of Disertori et al. We achieve this by establishing explicit, sharp lower bounds on the Green's functions fractional moments which imply that large moments of the position operator are infinite. Hence, these models exhibit Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization, that is, the absence of dynamical localization. In particular, this shows that in dimension $d=1$, the fractional Anderson model, a random perturbation of the fractional Laplacian $(-Δ)^α$ with $α\in (0,1)$, exhibits Poisson eigenvalue statistics and dynamical delocalization if the disorder is strong and the exponent $\frac{1}{2}<α<1$. This is in stark contrast with what is known for the usual Anderson model that has only nearest-neighbor hopping.

</details>


### [135] [Minimal output entropy for the channels that add or remove a box of a Young diagram, and a Pauli principle for every permutation symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33498)
*Robin Reuvers*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 U(d) 的两个仅相差一个方格的杨图表示之间由四个协变量子信道，证明相干态输出在所有输出中占优（majorize），从而极小化输出熵，并利用钩长公式显式计算最优输出；进一步给出保留方格的两个信道的全部极小元，并应用于 N 粒子置换对称性下的约化密度矩阵谱界与可达谱集合（如费米子的 Pauli 原理与 Coleman 定理）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示 U(d) 表示间的协变量子信道的输出优化问题，特别是确定哪些输入态能使输出熵极小，并将结果用于理解多粒子体系中约化密度矩阵谱的严格界限与可达性。

Method: 对差一个方格的杨图 U(d) 表示之间的四个协变量子信道（加/减方格 × 保留新图/保留方格即 C^d 或其对偶），证明相干态输出在 majorization 意义下占优任意输出；用钩长公式（hook lengths）显式计算最优输出；对保留方格的两个信道刻画全部极小元（不必是相干态，甚至不必是纯态）；应用层面分析 N 粒子置换对称性下单粒子约化密度矩阵的谱。

Result: 对每个信道，相干态输出 majorize 所有其他输出，从而相干态极小化输出熵；最优输出用钩长公式显式给出；保留方格的两个信道的全部极小元被完全确定（可为非相干纯态）；应用得到单粒子约化密度矩阵谱的锐界，以及混合态可达谱集合的精确刻画——费米子情形对应 Pauli 原理与 Coleman 定理。

Conclusion: 协变量子信道的熵极小元由相干态实现且可用组合数据（钩长）显式描述，极小元集合具有丰富结构（不限于相干态）；方法成功统一并推广了多粒子约化密度矩阵谱的经典结果（Pauli 原理、Coleman 定理），给出了锐的谱界与可达集合。

Abstract: Two irreducible representations of $U(d)$ whose Young diagrams differ by a single box are connected by four covariant quantum channels: the box can be removed or added, and one keeps either the new diagram or the box ($\mathbb{C}^d$ or its dual). We prove that for each of these channels the output of a coherent state majorizes every other output, so that coherent states minimize the output entropy, and we compute the optimal output explicitly in terms of hook lengths. For the two channels that keep the box we also determine all minimizers; these need not be coherent, even when they are pure. As an application we consider $N$ particles with a given permutation symmetry and find sharp bounds on the spectrum of the reduced density matrix of a single particle, as well as the exact set of spectra that mixed states can reach (for fermions these are the Pauli principle and Coleman's theorem).

</details>


### [136] [Effective atom-dimer bands of three-body lattice systems at strong coupling: exact critical mass ratios and a statistics-quasimomentum duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33576)
*Mikhail V. Dolgopolov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在方晶格上研究三粒子无限强度接触相互作用问题，对全布里渊区的准动量和三种统计（三玻色子、两费米子+一粒子、两玻色子+一粒子）系统求解原子-二聚体散射，通过费什巴赫-舒尔约化得到有效哈密顿量，将阈值问题化为4×4厄米矩阵判据，精确获得原子-二聚体区、临界质量比（闭合形式与pi/(pi-2)精确值）、至多两个束缚态及各种开始律，并利用重整化群解读与多重数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作仅处理了三聚体（trimer）和零准动量情形，而原子-二聚体（atom-dimer）散射在物理上对理解多体有效相互作用和束缚态至关重要，且需要覆盖整个布里渊区而非单个点；同时不同统计（玻色/费米）改变系统对称性与阈值的对应关系未被完整刻画，故需填补这一空白并给出严格可证明的结果。

Method: 通过费什巴赫-舒尔约化，将问题投影到对接触算符的无穷重简并本征值1上，得到具有一阶误差证明的显式有效哈密顿量；阈值问题进一步约化为由方晶格二点电阻构成的4×4厄米矩阵判据，等价于一个四次多项式的零点条件；结合解析求和、电脑代数、格点数值积分、精确对角化与无限晶格求解器交叉验证全部常数。

Result: 给出整个布里渊区上原子-二聚体区的闭合形式；证明临界质量比，其在零准动量处精确等于pi/(pi-2)并复现既有数值结果；证明对任意准动量和质量比至多存在两个原子-二聚体态；得到区边界上的本性奇点和纯p波中的“对数线性”类型的开始律；发现两个精确对偶：改变统计等价于准动量平移pi；并给出第二个费米阈值的全局范围。开始律可用重整化群解读：逆晶格格林函数是边缘s波耦合，束缚能通过晶格尺度为16的维度嬗变产生，区边界属边缘（BCS/Kondo）型而非Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless型。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了方晶格上三粒子无限强度接触相互作用问题的原子-二聚体散射，以严格且可证明的方式确定了全布里渊区的束缚态区、临界质量比、开始律和统计-动量对偶，并通过重整化群视角阐明其普适性机制；所有常数经多种独立数值方法验证，为少体物理与多体有效理论的衔接提供了精确基准。

Abstract: We study three particles on the square lattice with contact interactions of infinite strength, for all total quasimomenta, for three statistics: three identical bosons, two identical fermions plus a third particle, and two identical bosons plus a third particle. Previous work treated only the trimer and zero quasimomentum. Here the atom-dimer sector is analyzed on the whole Brillouin zone. A Feshbach-Schur reduction onto the infinitely degenerate eigenvalue 1 of the pair-contact operator gives explicit effective Hamiltonians with proved first-order error. The threshold problem reduces to a 4x4 Hermitian matrix criterion built from the two-point resistances of the square lattice, and hence to a quartic polynomial. This yields the complete atom-dimer zone and the critical mass ratio on the Brillouin zone, with bound between 1 and pi/(pi-2), and closed forms at all symmetry points. We prove: the zones in closed form; the critical mass ratio with exact value pi/(pi-2) at zero quasimomentum, reproducing a numerical value; at most two atom-dimer states for every quasimomentum and mass ratio; the onset laws, an essential singularity on the zone boundary and a linear-over-logarithm onset in the pure p-wave; two exact dualities showing that a change of statistics is equivalent to a shift of quasimomentum by pi; and the global range of the second fermionic threshold. The onset laws admit a renormalization-group reading: the inverse lattice Green function is a marginal s-wave coupling, binding energies arise by dimensional transmutation with lattice scale 16, and the zone boundary is of marginal (BCS/Kondo) type rather than of Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless type. All constants are verified independently by lattice quadrature, computer algebra, exact diagonalisation, and infinite-lattice solvers.

</details>


### [137] [$p$-adic spectral zeta functions via the inverse Stieltjes transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33660)
*Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 p-进谱 zeta 函数框架从离散谱推广到连续谱，利用逆 Stieltjes 变换从有界算子预解式构造 p-进谱分布，定义玻色与费米 zeta 函数及泛函行列式，并研究了它们的解析性质、特殊值公式与 Stirling 展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱 zeta 函数是量子物理中正则化微分算子行列式的标准工具。作者此前已为离散谱引入 p-进谱 zeta 函数，但该框架无法处理连续谱情形，因此需要将其推广到连续谱，以拓展 p-进量子力学中算子的正则化方法。

Method: 以有界算子的预解式（resolvent）为出发点，借助逆 Stieltjes 变换构造一个广义分布，称为 p-进谱分布；在此基础上定义玻色型与费米型 zeta 函数及对应的泛函行列式，并系统研究其解析性质、特殊值公式和 Stirling 展开；同时证明离散情形的旧框架可嵌入新框架。

Result: 建立了连续谱情形的 p-进谱分布，以及玻色和费米 zeta 函数与泛函行列式；得到了它们的解析性质、特殊值公式和 Stirling 展开；证明了此前的离散框架可嵌入新的连续框架；发现玻色与费米情形之间存在一种有趣的对称性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将 p-进谱 zeta 函数理论推广到连续谱，保留并扩展了原有离散结果，揭示玻色-费米对称性，并以任意紧子集上的 p-进量子力学位置算子作为应用，验证了新框架的有效性。

Abstract: Spectral zeta functions provide a standard tool for regularizing determinants of differential operators in quantum physics. In a previous paper (J. Math. Phys. 66: 083505, 2025), we introduced a $p$-adic spectral zeta function for discrete spectra via a locally analytic interpolation function. In this paper we extend the framework to continuous spectra using the inverse Stieltjes transform. Starting from the resolvent of a bounded operator, we construct a generalized distribution, called the $p$-adic spectral distribution, and define bosonic and fermionic zeta functions and functional determinants. We establish their analytic properties, special value formulas, and Stirling expansions, and show that our previous framework is embedded into the present one in the discrete case. In this approach, the bosonic and fermionic cases exhibit an interesting symmetry. As an application, we consider the position operator in $p$-adic quantum mechanics on an arbitrary compact subset of $\mathbb{C}_{p}$.

</details>


### [138] [Homogenized graphs with spectrally embedded defect states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33907)
*Kirill D. Cherednichenko,Stephen P. Shipman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过对高对比度周期度量图进行均质化，构造出能在连续谱中嵌入频率处产生由局部缺陷引起的束缚态的连续介质。关键机制在于将两个晶格图副本同时嵌入空间并通过边耦合，经傅里叶插值将图上的ODE均质化为空间中的PDE，得到具有内部自由度的有效介质，其杂交态空间可分解为两个正交子空间；临界高对比度保留微观共振，使一个子空间的谱带与另一个子空间的谱隙重叠，从而产生连续谱中嵌入的束缚态，且这些束缚态在ε尺度度量图上保持存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在均质化后的连续介质中产生“连续谱中嵌入的束缚态”（BIC），即在连续谱内由局部缺陷引起的束缚态，这类态通常难以在均质化极限下保留，论文旨在提供一种明确的构造机制。

Method: 通过将两个晶格图副本同时嵌入空间并用连接边耦合，实现对高对比度周期度量图的均质化；利用傅里叶分析进行插值，把图上的ODE算子表示到公共函数空间中，从而将ODE均质化为空间PDE；所得有效介质具有内部自由度，可分解为两个正交的杂交态空间；借助临界高对比度在有效介质中保留微观共振，并利用一个杂交态空间的谱带与另一杂交态空间的谱隙重叠来构造BIC；最后通过缺陷区域上约化预解式的O(ε)算子范数估计，证明BIC在ε尺度度量图上持续存在。

Result: 成功构造出由局部缺陷引起的、嵌入在连续谱中的束缚态，并证明这些连续谱中的束缚态对ε尺度度量图持续存在（由缺陷区域约化预解式的O(ε)算子范数估计保证）。

Conclusion: 高对比度周期图的均质化可以将微观共振保留到有效介质中，并利用耦合双晶格结构产生的内部自由度和叠加的谱带/谱隙机制，构造出嵌入连续谱的束缚态；这些束缚态在微观ε尺度上依然稳健存在。

Abstract: By homogenizing high-contrast periodic metric graphs, we create continuous media that admit bound states produced by local defects at energies embedded in the continuous spectrum. This is achieved by fitting two copies of a lattice graph simultaneously into space and coupling them by connecting edges. The homogenization of an ODE on a graph to a PDE in space is achieved by representing the associated operators in a common functional space through interpolation by Fourier analysis. The two coupled graphs result in an effective medium with internal degrees of freedom that allow a decomposition into two orthogonal spaces of hybrid states. Critical high contrast retains microscopic resonances in the effective medium and allows a spectral band of one hybrid state space to overlap a spectral gap of the other. This serves as the mechanism for creating bound states at frequencies embedded in the continuous spectrum of the effective medium. These bound states in the continuum persist for $\varepsilon$-scale metric graphs due to $O(\varepsilon)$ operator-norm estimates for reduced resolvents on the defect region.

</details>


### [139] [The equivalence of topological indices for $\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$-symmetric quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34119)
*Hal Tasaki*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Ogata 的算子代数拓扑指标与 Tasaki 的基本扭角（twist）指标在整数自旋链、在位 U(1)⋊Z₂ 对称且具有对称局域唯一有能隙基态的共同设定下等价，无需平移不变性或矩阵积表示。此外，将该等价用于反铁磁 Heisenberg 链，在显式唯一性与边界场能隙假设下确定了 Ogata 指标为 (-1)^S。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋链中的对称保护拓扑（SPT）相由拓扑指标区分。Ogata 的算子代数指标适用范围广、不要求连续对称性，而 Tasaki 的基本扭角指标则要求 U(1) 对称。两者在重叠的设定下应具有相同的物理含义，但此前缺乏直接等价性证明。本文旨在弥合这一差距，并给出可计算的公式。

Method: 在整数自旋链、在位 U(1)⋊Z₂ 对称和对称局域唯一有能隙基态的共同设定下（不假设平移不变性或矩阵积表示）证明两类指标等价。技术路线包括：(1) 通过电荷涨落估计给出半链旋转的强极限构造并固定其相位；(2) 利用精确的群交换子恒等式将 Ogata 指标与局部扭角期望的极限等同起来，并给出显式误差界。

Result: 证明了 Ogata 算子代数指标等于局部扭角期望的极限，并给出显式误差界，从而在已知能隙下界及相关基态期望时提供可计算公式。作为应用，利用之前的扭角指标计算结果，在显式唯一性与边界场能隙假设下确定反铁磁 Heisenberg 链的 Ogata 指标为 (-1)^S。

Conclusion: Ogata 指标与 Tasaki 扭角指标在共同适用范围内等价，且 Ogata 指标可转化为可计算的局部扭角期望极限公式。该结果为整数自旋链 SPT 相的分类提供了统一的、可计算的拓扑指标刻画，并成功应用于反铁磁 Heisenberg 链。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases in quantum spin chains are distinguished by topological indices. Ogata's operator-algebraic indices apply to a broad range of symmetry classes without requiring continuous symmetry, whereas the elementary twist index of Tasaki requires $\mathrm{U}(1)$ symmetry. We prove their equivalence in the common setting of integer-spin chains with on-site $\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry and a symmetric locally-unique gapped ground state, without assuming translation invariance or a matrix product representation. A charge-fluctuation estimate yields a strong-limit construction of half-chain rotations with their phases fixed. An exact group commutator identity then identifies the Ogata index with the limit of local twist expectations and gives an explicit error bound. This provides a computable formula when a lower bound on the gap and the relevant ground-state expectations are available. As an application, a previous twist-index calculation determines the Ogata index to be $(-1)^S$ for the antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chain under explicit uniqueness and boundary-field gap assumptions.
  The author formulated the problem and proposed the basic strategy, while ChatGPT made substantial contributions to the development of the proof.

</details>


### [140] [Discontinuity of Continuum Fermion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34402)
*Oliver Siebert*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究连续费米子在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 上通过配对势相互作用的 Heisenberg 动力学，证明了一大类非常数配对势下动力学即使在规范不变的 CAR 代数上也不逐点范数连续，从而说明 CAR 代数及其规范不变子代数在动力学下都不变，且非不变性对几乎所有时间成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解连续费米子系统在配对相互作用下 Heisenberg 动力学的代数结构与连续性性质，特别是当相互作用势非常数时是否破坏时间演化的逐点范数连续性以及代数不变性。

Method: 对一大类非常数配对势，在色散关系和外势满足适当假设的条件下，证明 Heisenberg 动力学即使在规范不变的 CAR 代数上也缺乏逐点范数连续性，再利用自动连续性（automatic-continuity）论证推出代数不在动力学下不变。

Result: 动力学在规范不变 CAR 代数上也不逐点范数连续，且 CAR 代数及其规范不变子代数在动力学下都不不变，非不变性对几乎所有的时刻都成立。

Conclusion: 在非常数配对势下，连续费米子的 Heisenberg 动力学具有强烈的非正则行为，导致时间演化无法将 CAR 代数或规范不变子代数映回自身，表明这类系统的动力学比无相互作用或常数势情形更为复杂。

Abstract: We study the Heisenberg dynamics of continuum fermions in $\IR^d$ interacting through a pair potential. For a large class of nonconstant pair potentials and under suitable assumptions on the dispersion relation and external potential, we prove that the dynamics fails to be pointwise-norm continuous even on the gauge-invariant CAR algebra. An automatic-continuity argument then shows that neither the CAR algebra nor its gauge-invariant subalgebra is invariant under the dynamics, with non-invariance occurring for almost all times.

</details>


### [141] [Classical BV Pushforward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34533)
*Xin Geng,Andrey Losev,Vyacheslav Lysov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义经典的BV推前（BV pushforward），并证明其两条核心性质：其一，它将总空间上经典主方程（CME）的解映射为较小空间上CME的解；其二，对孤立临界点情形，它将BV典范等价的解映射为BV典范等价的解。文中还通过例子说明该构造并讨论其应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种构造手段，将定义在较大空间上的BV理论与经典主方程的解系统地"降维"到较小的空间上，同时保持其解结构与等价类关系，以便在约化或商空间等背景下研究BV形式化。

Method: 作者定义经典的BV推前，即一种将总空间上的函数/对象推前到较小空间上的映射。核心工作在于证明两条性质：(1) 该推前使经典主方程（CME）解保持为CME解；(2) 在孤立临界点条件下，该推前保持BV典范等价（canonical equivalence）关系。随后用具体例子验证构造。

Result: 证明了BV推前将总空间上的CME解映射为较小空间上的CME解；并证明对孤立临界点，BV典范等价的解在推前后仍保持BV典范等价。

Conclusion: 经典BV推前是一个保持解结构与等价性的有效约化工具，可用于在较小空间上理解BV理论，并具有进一步的应用前景。

Abstract: We define the classical BV pushforward and establish its two main properties. First, we prove that it maps solutions of the classical master equation (CME) on the total space to solutions of the CME on the smaller space. Second, we prove that, for isolated critical points, it maps BV canonically equivalent solutions to BV canonically equivalent solutions. We illustrate the construction with examples and discuss its applications.

</details>


### [142] [Scattering Measures and Information Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34721)
*Tatsuro Oda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将散射实验形式化为结果空间上的参数化有限测度，并建立其 Fisher 度量下的信息几何，从而把实验操作、最优实验设计与量子测量的不完全探测统一在同一信息论框架内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统散射实验缺乏一套统一的信息几何描述，难以定量刻画测量过程对散射信息的影响，也难以将其与最优实验设计及量子测量中不完全探测的情形贯通。

Method: 将散射实验表示为结果空间上的参数化有限测度；利用 Fisher 度量构造信息几何；把测度分解为总质量（整体散射强度）与归一化形状（结果间的重分布），从而分离 Fisher 信息的两个贡献；将测量过程表示为作用于散射测度的概率或次概率核，并证明此类核算子会收缩 Fisher 信息。

Result: 建立了散射测度的 Fisher 几何，表明 Fisher 信息可分解为散射强度变化与结果重分布两部分；概率/次概率核对 Fisher 信息的收缩性为广泛的实验操作提供了统一的信息论刻画；并澄清了该有限测度结构在“量子测量—经典结果统计”层级中的位置。

Conclusion: 以参数化有限测度及 Fisher 度量为基础的散射实验信息几何，为散射实验、最优实验设计和不完全探测量子测量提供了统一的框架，并为测量过程中的信息损失提供了几何解释。

Abstract: We formulate scattering experiments in terms of parameterized finite measures on outcome spaces and develop the associated information geometry defined by the Fisher metric. The decomposition of a scattering measure into total mass and normalized shape separates Fisher information into contributions from changes in overall scattering strength and redistribution among outcomes. Measurement processes are represented by probability or sub-probability kernels acting on the scattering measure. Such kernels contract Fisher information, placing a broad class of experimental operations within a common information-theoretic framework. This formulation connects scattering experiments to the theory of optimal experimental design through the Fisher geometry of scattering measures. The same finite-measure structure arises naturally in quantum measurements with incomplete detection, clarifying its place in the hierarchy from quantum measurement to classical outcome statistics.

</details>


### [143] [Notes on Magnetostatic Interactions in $\mathbb R^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34766)
*Vladimir Gol'dshtein,Wolfgang H. Müller,Reuven Segev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无本构关系的紧凑磁静力学表述，通过电流密度势场定义磁偶极子分布，由势能表达式推导出包含洛伦兹力、开尔文力、非对称主动应力场及压强项的机械力泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不使用本构关系（包括以太关系）的前提下，建立磁偶极子分布区域的势能表达式，并由此推导机械力泛函，以避免传统方法中因依赖本构关系而产生的限制。

Method: 以电流密度势场刻画磁偶极子分布，在向量势场下给出该区域的势能表达式；假设偶极子分布虚运动时场做功等于势能时间导数的负值，据此推导出机械力泛函。

Result: 所得机械力泛函除洛伦兹力密度和开尔文力密度外，还包含一个非对称主动应力场（其反对称部分为机械力偶密度）以及一个压强项。

Conclusion: 该紧凑磁静力学表述无需本构关系即可构造势能并推导完整的机械力泛函，揭示了洛伦兹力、开尔文力、主动应力与压强项的内在构成。

Abstract: A compact formulation of magnetostatics is presented. Without using constitutive relations, including the aether relations, an expression is proposed for the potential energy of a region carrying a distribution of magnetic dipoles under a vector potential field, where the dipole distribution is specified by a current density potential field. Assuming that during a virtual motion of the dipole distribution the virtual work expended by the field is equal to minus the time derivative of the potential energy, an expression for the mechanical force functional is derived. In addition to the Lorentz and Kelvin force densities, the force functional contains an asymmetric active stress field, the skew-symmetric part of which is the mechanical couple density, and a pressure term.

</details>


### [144] [A renormalized Lee Model: resolvent and spectral analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34980)
*Claudio Cacciapuoti,Federica Muscolino,Diego Noja,Andrea Posilicano*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造并分析了一个重整化的三维大质量 Lee 模型，通过抽象的 Krein 型预解式公式定义重整化哈密顿量，利用激发数守恒将谱分析化为对算子丛（算子值 Weyl 函数）的分扇区研究，并证明了分立谱与本质谱的若干定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lee 模型是量子场论中可精确求解且具有重整化现象的重要模型。三维大质量情形的重整化哈密顿量通常缺乏严格的内在定义，其谱结构（本质谱的 HVZ 型描述、阈值下的点谱与束缚态存在性）也缺乏系统的数学刻画。

Method: 作者通过抽象构造显式给出重整化哈密顿量的算子域、作用方式与 Krein 型预解式公式；利用总激发数守恒，将全哈密顿量的谱分析约化为对每个扇区中某个显式算子丛（扮演算子值 Weyl 函数角色）的谱轮廓研究；结合 Birman–Schwinger 原理与弱耦合展开等手段进行分析。

Result: 在由较低激发扇区生成的扇区中构造出本质谱分量，并在前两个扇区（以及在显式条件下的每个扇区）证明期望的扇区式 HVZ 型公式；在自由阈值或单激发阈值之下，每个较高扇区的点谱由一个非负有界自伴的 Birman–Schwinger 算子描述；在每个至少含两个激发的扇区中，当重整化能隙不小于玻色子质量时对任意耦合证明不存在阈下谱，当能隙较小时在显式弱耦合条件下同样证明不存在阈下谱，并在后者情形下给出双激发扇区存在简单束缚态的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该抽象构造为三维大质量 Lee 模型的重整化哈密顿量提供了严格的内在定义与预解式，并将谱问题系统化为分扇区的算子丛分析，从而获得 HVZ 型本质谱公式、阈下点谱的 Birman–Schwinger 刻画以及束缚态存在与否的定性判据。

Abstract: We construct and analyze a renormalized three-dimensional massive Lee model; its renormalized Hamiltonian is defined intrinsically through an abstract construction which explicitly provides the operator domain, action and a Kre\uın-like resolvent formula. Conservation of the total excitation number reduces the spectral analysis of the full Hamiltonian to a sector-wise study of the spectral profile of an explicit operator pencil, playing the role of an operator-valued Weyl function. We construct essential spectrum components in a sector generated by lower excitation sectors and obtain the inclusion in the expected sector-wise HVZ type formula; the full formula is proved in the first two sectors and, under explicit conditions, in every sector. Below the free or one-excitation threshold, the point spectrum in every higher sector is described by a bounded self-adjoint nonnegative Birman--Schwinger operator. In every sector with at least two excitations we prove absence of sub-threshold spectrum for arbitrary coupling when the renormalized gap is not smaller than the boson mass, and under an explicit weak-coupling condition when it is smaller. In the latter case we also derive a sufficient condition for the existence of a simple bound state in the two-excitation sector.

</details>


### [145] [GR-FM: Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching for SDF-Based Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35006)
*Yuxin Ai,Zhichang Guo,Fanghui Song,Dazhi Zhang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出GR-FM，一种基于图像条件、几何正则化的流匹配框架，将医学图像分割建模为朝向符号距离场(SDF)表示的概率传输过程，并通过双调和正则项与Eikonal约束提升几何与边界精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流匹配的生成式分割方法主要在状态空间学习速度场，缺乏对恢复表示的空间正则性和距离场属性的显式约束，容易导致空间振荡、边界位移及细结构不连续；同时医学图像存在弱边界、细粒度结构和高质量标注稀缺等难题。

Method: 提出图像条件的几何正则化流匹配框架GR-FM。将分割形式化为从初始分布到目标隐式表示分布的连续概率传输过程；采用SDF描述目标结构（每个像素以其到边界的符号距离表示，形成连续几何场）；利用流匹配与常微分方程(ODE)实现确定性、高效的分布传输；训练目标引入双调和正则项与Eikonal约束以增强空间平滑性、结构一致性和距离场特性；并分析了几何约束下的连续传输过程及零水平集的演化。

Result: 在MoNuSeg、GlaS和DRIVE三个数据集上的实验表明，GR-FM在区域重叠度和边界精度上均取得有竞争力的性能，降低了性能波动，并仅用少量积分步数即可保持稳定的分割结果。

Conclusion: GR-FM通过将符号距离场与几何正则化的流匹配相结合，有效缓解了空间振荡与边界位移问题，在医学图像分割中实现了高效、稳定且边界精确的性能。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation remains challenging in terms of accurate boundary localization and complex-structure preservation, as target regions often exhibit weak boundaries, fine-grained structures, and irregular shapes, while high-quality images and precise annotations are usually limited. Existing generative segmentation approaches based on Flow Matching mainly learn velocity fields in the state space but lack explicit constraints on the spatial regularity and distance-field properties of the recovered representation. This limitation may lead to spatial oscillations, boundary displacement, and discontinuities in fine structures. To address these issues, we propose an image-conditioned Geometrically Regularized Flow Matching framework, termed GR-FM, which formulates segmentation as a continuous probability transport process from an initial distribution to a target implicit representation distribution. Instead of directly modeling binary masks, GR-FM adopts the SDF to describe the target structure, where each pixel is represented by its signed distance to the object boundary, resulting in a continuous geometric field. Flow Matching and ordinary differential equations are then employed to achieve deterministic and efficient distribution transport. The training objective further incorporates a biharmonic regularization term and an Eikonal constraint to enhance the spatial smoothness, structural consistency, and distance-field characteristics of the recovered representation. Moreover, we analyze the continuous transport process under geometric constraints and investigate the evolution of the zero level set. Experiments conducted on the MoNuSeg, GlaS, and DRIVE datasets demonstrate that GR-FM achieves competitive performance in both region overlap and boundary accuracy, reduces performance variation, and maintains stable segmentation results with only a small number of integration steps.
